List all three models in CREDITS.md (Meshy-generated from owner art).
- **R7.B3 In the match.** Wherever Grubnak is a Commander (player or enemy), the old Grubnak figure is replaced by the Meshy model standing on the Meshy fortress, with the scepter attached to his right-hand bone (moves with his animations). Total height (fortress + Grubnak) about **2.4** board spaces, footprint 1 × 1 on the Stronghold space, facing the opponent. Owner camera and HUD layout unchanged; nothing covers him (AC151/AC166 still pass). Animations: **idle** loops all the time; **cast** when he casts a spell (any spell his side plays); **hit** when his Stronghold/Commander takes damage; **laugh** or **point** (random, sometimes) when one of his creatures defeats an enemy; **celebrate** when his side wins; **defeat** when his side loses (surrender included). Each one-shot returns to idle afterwards (except celebrate/defeat at match end).
  - **Test hook:** `getRenderStats()` gains `commanderModels`: `[{ side: "player"|"opponent", commanderId, source, height, animations, currentAnimation, animationLog, fortress, scepter, scepterBone }]` with one entry per Commander, where for a Meshy Grubnak `source` = `"grubnak.glb"`, `height` = rendered total height in spaces, `animations` = the canonical names available (all 8), `currentAnimation` = canonical name playing now, `animationLog` = canonical names of one-shot animations played since the match started, in order, `fortress` = `"grubnak-fortress.glb"`, `scepter` = `"grubnak-scepter.glb"`, `scepterBone` = the name of the bone the scepter is parented to (a right-hand bone). Other Commanders report `source: "placeholder"` or their current model. `commanders[side].model` stays as before.
- **R7.B4 3D viewer.** On **Choose Your Commander** and in the **Deck Builder** there is a plain-text button **"View Grubnak in 3D"** (same text style as the other menu buttons). It opens a `role="dialog"` named **"Grubnak in 3D"** with a WebGL canvas showing the model on his fortress, plain-text buttons **Idle, Laugh, Point, Celebrate, Surprised, Cast, Hit, Defeat** (each plays that animation) and **Close**. Drag spins the model, scroll or pinch zooms. Esc also closes. On the select screen, opening the viewer does not change the chosen Commander.
  - **Test hook:** `window.oreheart.getViewerState()` → `{ open, source, animation, rotationY, zoom }` (`source` = `"grubnak.glb"`, `animation` = canonical name playing now, `rotationY` in radians, `zoom` = camera distance or zoom factor).
- **R7.B5 Report:** screenshots of Grubnak in a match at 1920 × 1080 and in the 3D viewer (AC184 saves them), plus the Meshy credits spent (from `models.json`).

### Phase 1B, Round 6a (owner playtest of 5b: match fixes, card back, text palette, deck builder) — done

Source: the "Round 6a" section at the top of `factory-design/design/oreheart-phase-1b-brief.md` and the updated GDD (hand tucked halfway, text palette, card backs, no corner ornaments). Both are binding. Keep everything from Round 5b and earlier unless an item below replaces it; **rules are unchanged**. **Not in this round:** Round 6b / Build B (Meshy 3D models, starting with Grubnak). Where this section conflicts with older text (R5b.7 "Done horizontally centered", R5b.4 hand count / "nearly fully visible" hand / enemy hand at 29 % / enemy deck at 34 % / Your deck at 83 % / rose #f0cdbf name lines, R5a.1 "all text plain golden yellow", R3b "gold filigree ornaments framing every screen", the Deck Builder close-up's Close button, the "All cards" label), **this section wins**. New criteria: AC161–AC173.

- **R6a.1 Done bottom-right.** "Done" (still shown only when needed, R5b.7, plain bright-gold text, Bold) sits in the **bottom-right corner directly under "Your deck"**: its top is at or below the deck pile's bottom edge (≤ 4 px overlap), its horizontal center lies within the deck pile's left/right edges, its center is right of 80 % of the width, and it is fully inside the window. "Your deck" moves up as needed (its top between 62 % and 84 % of the height) so Done fits without shrinking. Done is never in the middle of the screen. **Nothing invisible blocks the board:** in the match, any point of the window that is not covered by a visible HUD piece hits the Battlefield (`document.elementFromPoint` returns the element with `aria-label="Battlefield"` or one of its descendants, e.g. the canvas or a creature/mine tap target). No empty full-screen overlays, hit areas or containers with pointer events. In particular every point in the central region (35–65 % of the width, 30–60 % of the height) hits the Battlefield whenever no dialog, reveal or banner is showing.
- **R6a.2 No hand counts.** Remove the player's "N cards" text and the enemy's hand count: no element labelled "Hand count" or "Enemy hand count" and no visible text like "5 cards" on the match screen. The deck piles' cards-left numbers stay (AC44).
- **R6a.3 Player's hand 2× and tucked halfway.** *(Round 10 R10.9: now tucked to a quarter, 18–32 % visible.)* Each hand card is about **46 %** of the window height (between 38 % and 54 % at every window shape, never stretched). The hand is **horizontally centered** (the hand container's center between 44 % and 56 % of the width). Cards fan with roughly **half-card overlap** (each card's left edge 40–60 % of a card width after the previous one), so about 7 cards fit in about two-thirds of the width; more cards scroll/swipe (AC115). **At rest** the hand is tucked halfway below the bottom edge: between 35 % and 65 % of each card is visible above the bottom edge. Hovering the hand (or tapping it on touch) slides it **fully up** (whole cards visible, bottom edge ≤ window bottom); moving away tucks it back. While a card or creature is being dragged, the hand drops out of the way (at most 25 % of a card visible).
- **R6a.4 Hover pop-out 2×.** The hovered (or held) hand card itself becomes the pop-out: a big readable close-up whose height is **85–90 % of the window height** (tests accept 80–92 %), proportions kept, fully on-screen (never covering the whole screen), drawn on the **very top layer**: `document.elementFromPoint` at its center and at points 25 % in from each of its corners returns the popped card or a descendant (no neighboring card, HUD piece or board on top). It still counts as the AC59 "doubles in size" pop-out.
- **R6a.5 Original Oreheart card back.** Every face-down card (enemy hand cards, both deck piles, mulligan/deck animations) shows one new picture: the OREHEART logo (from `references/main logo.jpg`) in the middle of a dark, rich back with a gold filigree border and a glowing crystal motif, no other text, not resembling any other game's back. Generated with the image API, stored as `public/art/ui/oreheart-card-back.(webp|png|jpg)`, listed in CREDITS.md. Each face-down element keeps `data-card-back` and displays that file either as an `<img>` (src contains `oreheart-card-back`) inside it or as its CSS `background-image` (url contains `oreheart-card-back`). The old purple back is removed from the game. The in-match deck piles ("Your deck", "Enemy deck") now show this card back (each contains at least one `[data-card-back]`) instead of the R2 deck image; the Deck Builder pile and the select-screen deck stacks keep the deck image (AC93).
- **R6a.6 Enemy hand centered at the top; enemy deck to its left.** The enemy hand (`aria-label="Enemy hand"`) is horizontally centered at the top (its center within 6 % of the width of the player's hand center and between 42 % and 58 % of the width), card backs peeking down from the top edge (top ≤ 6 % of the height). The **enemy deck** pile (`aria-label="Enemy deck"`, with its cards-left number) sits **just to the left** of the enemy hand: its right edge at most 2 % of the width right of the enemy hand's left edge and at most 12 % of the width left of it, top ≤ 2 %. **Nothing covers either Commander** (`screenRects()`; ≤ 10 % overlap for every HUD piece, including Done and the resting hand) at any window shape. Move the enemy crystal sideways (its left edge between 58 % and 76 % of the width) and keep the phase tracker at the top-right (right edge ≥ 96 % of the width) so their **text never overlaps** (checked with the text rectangles of both pieces), also at 844 × 390.
- **R6a.7 Shared text palette and weights**, defined once as CSS custom properties on `:root` (exact names; values case-insensitive):
  - `--text-gold: #FFD700` — titles, screen headings, main actions (Menu, Done, Fight!, menu buttons), important numbers.
  - `--text-brown: #B8862B` and `--text-brown-light: #D4A94F` — secondary labels, section headings, captions, inactive tabs, flavor lines.
  - `--text-white: #FFFFFF` and `--text-soft: #F4ECDC` — body text, rules text, list entries, numbers on crystals and hearts (the R5b rose #f0cdbf name/Next lines become `--text-soft`).
  - `--text-black: #000000` and `--text-ink: #1A1208` — outlines and shadows behind light text; text on gold or light surfaces.
  - Meaning colors, also on `:root`: `--color-player` (blue), `--color-enemy` (red), `--color-warning` (orange-red), `--color-success` (green), `--color-damage: #ff4040` (damaged Health, Revision 2), and the faction colors `--color-goblins: #3ee06a`, `--color-gnomes: #3fa9ff`, `--color-dwarves: #b45cff`; the first four values are the builder's choice.
  - **No stray text colors:** on the title, select, Deck Builder, Rules, Settings and match screens, every visible text's computed `color` has the RGB of one of these 15 variables (alpha/opacity may vary, e.g. dimmed or disabled). Text inside card faces (`[data-card-id]`, `[data-card-back]`) keeps the card designs' own faction colors and is exempt, as is `aria-hidden` decoration. All text buttons (menu buttons, in-match controls, Deck Builder actions) stay `--text-gold` (keeps AC137/AC157).
  - **Weights** (Alegreya SC Italic; bundle the 400, 500, 700 and 800 italic files): **700–800** for titles, screen headings (`h1`/`h2`), card names, Commander names, big numbers and main actions (Menu, Done, Fight!, the title menu buttons); **500** for labels and other buttons; **400** for body and rules text (e.g. the Rules page paragraphs). Computed weights are only 400, 500, 700 or 800. Check contrast at 844 × 390.
- **R6a.8 Deck Builder card close-up.** A single click on a card tile opens the close-up (still `role="dialog"` named after the card, containing its `[data-card-id]` face): the card fills the window **top to bottom** with tiny margins (face height ≥ 90 % and ≤ 100 % of the window height), proportions kept (same width/height ratio as the tile, ± 3 %), centered, **no scrollbar of any kind** (the dialog, its descendants and the page are not scrollable: `scrollHeight ≤ clientHeight + 1` and `scrollWidth ≤ clientWidth + 1`), and **no Close button**. A click on the card, a click anywhere else, or **Esc** dismisses it. (Other dialogs, e.g. the in-match card view of AC22/AC98 and Last turn, keep their Close buttons.)
- **R6a.9 Deck list "+" fix.** "+" ("Add NAME") behaves exactly like "−" ("Remove NAME"): the count changes on the very next frame, no animation or transition on the row or list, no re-sort jump and no layout shift (fixed-width counts and buttons: after a click the row's "+" and "−" buttons stay within 1 px of where they were, as long as the row is not removed), and rapid repeated clicks all register (5 fast clicks = +5).
- **R6a.10 Remove the "All cards" label** on the left of the Deck Builder: no visible text "All cards". The card grid keeps its `aria-label="All cards"` (screen readers, tests).
- **R6a.11 No corner ornaments.** Remove the rings / corner ornaments in the four corners of the Deck Builder and every other screen (cancels R3b "ornaments frame every screen"): no element with `data-ornament="frame"` or `data-ornament="corner"` exists on the title, select, Deck Builder, Rules, Settings or match screens, and no visible decorative image sits in a window corner. Filigree on cards and the rest of the gold style stay.
- **R6a.12 Owner's placeholder crystal mine model.** Copy `factory-design/design/models/crystal-mine-placeholder.gltf` into the game's assets and load it with three.js `GLTFLoader` for every crystal mine (replacing the procedural crystals). Scale it to about **1.5** spaces tall (the file is ≈ 1.32 tall, origin at base center, Y-up, faces +Z). Tint by owner by swapping the crystal materials' color and emissive: **blue** for the player, **red** for the enemy, **pale/neutral** when unowned; keep the glow and flow particles. The model path is one constant (one-line swap for a later Meshy model). List it in CREDITS.md as owner-supplied.
  - **Test hook:** `getRenderStats()` gains `mineModels`: `[{ row, col, source, height, owner, tint }]` for each of the 3 mines, where `source` = the loaded file name (`"crystal-mine-placeholder.gltf"`), `height` = rendered world height in spaces, `owner` = `"player" | "opponent" | "neutral"` (as currently shown), `tint` = the crystal color as `"#rrggbb"`. Player tint has blue > red, opponent tint has red > blue, all neutral mines share one tint that differs from both. `mines` stays `"finished"`.
- **R6a.13 Leftovers.** The income preview text is **"Next +N"** everywhere (AC36 updated from "+0 next turn"). Repaint the ruby hearts with the image API (painted art, not drawn in code; same file `public/art/ui/ruby-heart.(png|webp)`, AC159). The Menu's "Surrender" item stays. Tutorial tips and the Rules page that mention the hand, Done or hand counts match this layout.

**Report:** include screenshots of a match at 1920 × 1080 and 844 × 390 (hand at rest, hand hovered with a pop-out), the Deck Builder with a card close-up, and a card back (AC173 saves them as test outputs).

### Phase 1B, Round 5b (match screen: owner camera and layout) — done

Source: the "Round 5b" section at the top of `factory-design/design/oreheart-phase-1b-brief.md` (it contains the owner's exact camera-fit code; copy it) and the updated GDD (hand at the bottom, Last turn in the pop-out Menu, owner layout wins). Both are binding. Keep everything from Round 5a and earlier unless an item below replaces it; **rules are unchanged**. **Not in this round:** Build B (Meshy 3D models). Where this section conflicts with older text (R2.10/R2.11 grouped HUD, R3.9 HUD around the corners, R9.7 "Last turn" top-left, R2.19 "Re-center", the Round 2 "Current phase top center", Revision 8 tucked hand, R2 "ornate" Done), **this section wins**. All percentages are of the window width (x) / height (y), measured from the top-left of each piece; the HUD scales with the window.

- **R5b.1 Camera (owner's exact numbers). Replaced in Round 8a by R8a.1 (painted arena, cover-fit view); kept here for history.** Perspective camera looking at the board center from **51°** above the ground with a **48°** vertical field of view; fit exactly as the brief's `place`/`ext` code: binary-search the distance at which the full board width and both Commanders (head to feet, standing on their Strongholds) fit, then zoom to **107 %**, leave **16 %** of the height as painted sky / distant mountains above the far side, shift the board image **up 12 %** and **right 1 %**, bottom margin 2 %. Re-fit with the same rules for every window shape (16:9, 16:10, 21:9, 4:3, 844 × 390 phone) and live on resize; if a HUD piece would then cover a Commander, nudge the fit (never the HUD positions). Both Commanders are always completely on-screen. The match canvas always fills the whole window: **no black bars** at any shape (sky, haze and ground paint every pixel). Polished animated-film light: warm key light, soft shadows, distance haze, glowing crystals. Zoom and pan stay (R2.19); the reset button is now **"Recenter"** (in the Menu, R5b.4) and returns exactly to these numbers.
  - **Test hook:** `window.oreheart.getCamera()` → `{ zoom, panX, panY, tilt, fov, fitZoom, sky, shiftX, shiftY }`: `zoom`/`panX`/`panY` are the player's own zoom/pan as before (1/0/0 after Recenter); `tilt` = **51**, `fov` = **48** (degrees, the camera's real values at zoom 1), `fitZoom` = **1.07**, `sky` = **0.16**, `shiftX` = **0.01**, `shiftY` = **0.12**.
  - **Test hook:** `window.oreheart.screenRects()` → `{ playerCommander, opponentCommander, board }`, each `{ x, y, width, height }` in CSS pixels of the window: the screen-space bounding box of the projected 3D figure (Commander + Stronghold, feet to head top) and of the 13 × 7 board's four corners.
- **R5b.2 Figure sizes** (1 space = 1 unit): creatures about **1.1** spaces tall; each Commander standing on his Stronghold about **2.4** spaces tall in total; crystal mines about **1.5** tall with a strong glow. Commanders still occupy one space.
- **R5b.3 Soul crystal flow particles:** a mine you own streams glowing particles from the top of its crystals in a smooth curving ribbon into your Stronghold (your color); enemy-owned mines stream into the enemy's Stronghold; neutral mines swirl gently above the crystals. Streams redirect when a mine changes owner and pulse brighter when crystals are collected at the start of a turn. GPU particles, capped, drawn under units and the HUD. Settings has a radio group **"Flow particles"** with **"On"**, **"Reduced"**, **"Off"** (default On; Reduced is chosen automatically on slow devices; saved in localStorage).
- **R5b.4 HUD layout (owner's positions; tolerance a few %).** No boxes behind any HUD piece.
  - **Your crystal** (`aria-label="Your crystal"`), top-left at (0 %, 1 %): a large glowing faceted crystal image (`role="img"`, name **"Your soul crystal"**). **ON the crystal:** your Flow number big, white with a dark outline (`aria-label="Your Flow crystals"`, text = the number), and just below it the **bank badge**: a small gold bank-building icon with the banked number on top of it, number larger than the icon (`aria-label="Your Bank crystals"`, text = the number). Both labels' centers lie inside the crystal image's box. **Beside** the crystal (to its right): your Commander's display name, the word **"Flow"** in golden yellow, and **"Next +N"** (`aria-label="Your income next turn"`, the existing income preview). Name and Next lines in warm pale rose **#f0cdbf**. No separate Bank or Mines lines.
  - **Enemy crystal** (`aria-label="Enemy crystal"`) at (70 %, 0 %), slightly smaller, same design in red: image **"Enemy soul crystal"**, **"Enemy Flow crystals"** and **"Enemy Bank crystals"** on it, the enemy Commander's name, "Flow" and **"Enemy income next turn"** ("Next +N") beside it.
  - **Phase tracker** (`aria-label="Current phase"`, as before) at the top-right (80 %, 0 %): first line **"Your turn · Turn N"** (on the enemy's turn **"Enemy turn · Turn N"** in red), then **Draw › Deploy › Action › End** with the current phase lit (that word's element has `aria-current="step"`), finished ones dimmed, and the time left below (when shown); all right-aligned with a soft dark fade behind it on the right (a gradient, not a box). The big center phase banners stay.
  - **Enemy hand** (`aria-label="Enemy hand"`): card backs peeking down from the top edge at (29 %, 3 %), about 1.3× their Round 5a size (still smaller than your hand cards, AC105); a small card count to its lower-left.
  - **Enemy deck** (`aria-label="Enemy deck"`): a pile peeking from the top edge at (34 %, −3 %), with the cards-left number on it, no label text.
  - **Menu** at the right side (89 %, 15 %): only the word **"Menu"** (a `<button>` named "Menu", `aria-expanded`) shows. Hovering it (or tapping/clicking it) slides out, below it, **"Last turn"** (button), **"Mute"** (button with `aria-pressed`, toggles all sound, remembered), the **"Card pop-ups"** switch (visible text "Pop-ups On" / "Pop-ups Off", still `role="switch"` named "Card pop-ups", R2.15) and **"Recenter"** (button), plus **"Surrender"** last (needed for AC20). When the pointer leaves the Menu and its panel, the items hide again (not visible); a click/tap on "Menu" opens it and keeps it open until "Menu" is clicked again, the player clicks elsewhere, or an item is used. All plain text with a hover glow, no boxes (R5a.3 style).
  - **Your deck** (`aria-label="Your deck"`) at the bottom-right (93 %, 83 %): a pile showing your deck picture with the cards-left number on it, no label.
  - **Your hand** (`aria-label="Your hand"`) along the bottom, its first card's left edge at **14 %** of the width; each card about **23 %** of the window height; resting **nearly fully visible** (at most 15 % of a card below the bottom edge) — this replaces the Revision 8 tucked hand. Hovering or tapping pops it out: the hand rises fully and the hovered card enlarges (the 2× pop-out of AC59), always fully on-screen. "N cards" (`aria-label="Hand count"`) sits small to the right of the hand. About 7 cards shown, swipe/scroll for more (AC115).
  - No HUD piece (crystals, phase tracker, enemy hand, enemy deck, Menu word, your deck, resting hand) covers either Commander's figure (`screenRects()`); nudge the camera fit if needed. The Commander hearts stay on the Stronghold walls (R6.7).
- **R5b.5 Playable cards glow:** each hand card button has `data-playable="true"` when it can be played right now (legal and affordable in the current phase, i.e. `legalActions()` contains an action using its uid) and `"false"` otherwise; playable cards show a glowing golden halo (`box-shadow` or `filter: drop-shadow(...)` on the card button or its card face), the others have neither. Updates immediately after every action or phase change.
- **R5b.6 Health hearts:** the HUD and on-board hearts become a glossy painted ruby heart picture (generated with the image API, no text in the picture, stored as `public/art/ui/ruby-heart.(png|webp)` and listed in CREDITS.md) with the number in white with a thin dark outline. The hearts painted in the owner's card frames stay.
- **R5b.7 In-match controls as text:** Done, Respond, Pass, the mulligan choices ("Keep Hand", "Mulligan …"), the pause menu (Surrender confirm) and the match-end summary buttons ("Play again", "Menu") are plain gold text with a hover glow whose color changes, no border/background/box-shadow/outline (same checks as AC137). **"Done" is shown only when needed:** only during the human player's own Deploy or Action phase while they have priority and no dialog (mulligan, Respond, confirm) is open; otherwise it is not rendered or hidden. It floats horizontally centered on the screen (its center between 40 % and 60 % of the width), in the lower middle above the hand.
- **R5b.8 Card reveals 3× longer:** setting `revealSeconds` in `game-config.json` = **5.5** (Normal); "Card reveals: Fast" uses half (about 2.75 s). The game keeps going underneath; tap to pin or dismiss (AC97 unchanged).
- **R5b.9 Readable sizes:** at 844 × 390 no visible text on the title, select, Deck Builder, Settings, Rules or match screen has a computed font size below **14 px** (checked ≥ 13.5), except text inside card faces (`[data-card-id]`, `[data-card-back]`), which scale with the card picture and are read through the pop-out, and `aria-hidden` decoration. Where space is short (deck builder list/chart on a phone) show fewer rows and scroll instead of shrinking text.
- **R5b.10 Tutorial / Rules:** tutorial tips and the Rules page that mention where things are on the screen must match this layout (the `savings` tip still mentions "Last turn", now "in the Menu").

**Report:** include screenshots of a match at 1920 × 1080, 2560 × 1080 (21:9) and 844 × 390, plus the Menu popped out and the hand popped out (AC160 saves them as test outputs).

### Phase 1B, Round 5a (menus, deck builder, lettering) — done

Source: the "Round 5a" section at the top of `factory-design/design/oreheart-phase-1b-brief.md` and the updated GDD (deck builder layout, "cards are never stretched", lettering). Both are binding. Keep everything from earlier rounds unless an item below replaces it; **rules are unchanged**. **Not in this round:** Round 5b (match screen: perspective camera, corner layout, crystal display, hearts, hand size, flow particles, longer reveals) and Build B (Meshy 3D models). Where this section conflicts with older text (R4.5 metal lettering, the Look & feel fonts, R3b.6/R2 "ornate buttons" on menu screens, R2/R3b Deck Builder layout, R4.4 temporary spell frames), **this section wins**. New criteria: AC135–AC148. Retired: AC133 (replaced by AC135). Fixed checks: AC72, AC74, AC130 (and AC86's font names).

- **R5a.1 Lettering (replaces R4.5).** Remove the metal text textures and every metal/texture fill on letters: no element uses `background-clip: text`, and nothing references `metal-gold`/`metal-silver`. **All text** (names, titles, buttons, numbers, card text, menus, rules, settings, battle words such as KA-BOOM!, in DOM and, where practical, on the canvas) uses one font: **"Alegreya SC", Italic, weight 400** (weight 700 italic only where small text fails readability on a phone; no other weights). Self-host the font files (e.g. `public/assets/external/fonts/alegreya-sc-*.woff2`, from Google Fonts, SIL OFL) with `@font-face`; **no request to fonts.googleapis.com or fonts.gstatic.com** at runtime; list "Alegreya SC" in `CREDITS.md`. Color: plain solid golden yellow **#FFD700** (`rgb(255, 215, 0)`) with only a thin dark outline / soft text-shadow; flavor text may be a paler gold; existing exceptions stay (Health numbers on hearts white, damaged Health and deck problems red, greyed-out states). Testable: on the title, Rules, Settings, Deck Builder, select and match screens every visible text node with a letter/digit (ignoring `aria-hidden` subtrees, canvas, svg and text boxes) has computed `font-family` starting with `Alegreya SC`, `font-style: italic`, `font-weight` 400 or 700; `document.fonts.check('italic 400 20px "Alegreya SC"')` is true; the title-screen menu buttons and the "Deck Builder" heading are exactly `rgb(255, 215, 0)`.
- **R5a.2 Cards are never stretched (permanent rule).** Every card face everywhere (hand, mulligan, reveal, card view, Deck Builder grid at any cards-per-row, Respond, drag ghost) keeps its frame's exact proportions; extra space goes between/around cards. Testable: for every visible element with `data-card-id` that contains its frame `<img>` (src containing `frame`), the face's layout size (`offsetWidth/offsetHeight`) and the frame img's layout size both match the frame image's natural aspect ratio within 3 %, and no ancestor applies a non-uniform scale. The frame image fills the face exactly. (The owner's frames are 1152 × 1712.)
- **R5a.3 Menu buttons become text** on every menu screen (title, Choose Your Commander / deck picker, Deck Builder, Settings, Rules, pause and match-end menus): still real `<button>`s with the same names, but at rest **no border, no background color or image, no box-shadow, no outline**; text color `#FFD700`. Hover: soft gold glow behind the word (text-shadow and/or a radial background **only while hovered**), text brightens and grows slightly, `cursor: pointer`; quick flash on press. A selected choice (e.g. the chosen difficulty) is shown by a stronger text glow, still without a box. Sliders, toggles, dropdowns and scrollbars keep their ornate gold style (AC122). In-match controls are unchanged until round 5b. Card-like buttons (Commander cards, deck stacks, card tiles) are not "menu buttons".
- **R5a.4 Menu screens fill the window and scale live.** Each menu screen's root element carries **`data-screen`** = `"title"`, `"select"`, `"deck-builder"`, `"settings"` or `"rules"` and always covers the window exactly (top-left at 0,0; width/height = the window's, ±2 px) at any size, re-laying out live on resize (no reload). The page itself never scrolls (`documentElement` scroll size ≤ window size); lists scroll inside their panels. Checked at 844×390, 1280×720, 1920×1080 and while resizing.
- **R5a.5 Deck Builder layout** (replaces earlier Deck Builder layout text; all names/labels used by older tests stay):
  - **Left side:** the card grid ("All cards") with the controls above it, all left-aligned: "Search cards" searchbox, **"Cards per row" slider (`input type="range"`, `min="2"`, `max="8"`)**, Faction/Type/Rarity/Cost dropdowns and "Deck Commander". At N per row exactly N cards share the first row.
  - **Logo:** the OREHEART logo (`img` named "Oreheart") about 3× bigger, at the top, horizontally in the middle area (its center between 20 % and 65 % of the window width), top within 15 % of the height, at least 12 % of the window height tall, never overlapping the search box, the slider or the right panel.
  - **Right panel:** an element labelled **`aria-label="Deck panel"`**, starting at the very top edge (top ≤ 2 px), in the right part of the window (left edge ≥ 45 % of the width), everything left-aligned (left edges of the title, deck name, first action, Back and the "Deck list" heading within 24 px of each other). From top to bottom:
    1. The **"Deck Builder"** heading, about 3× (≥ 48 px at 1920×1080), the largest text in the panel.
    2. The **"Deck pile"** block (`aria-label="Deck pile"`, also the drop target) containing the **deck carousel** (`aria-label="My decks"`) and the selected deck's name and count. No border/box/background around the deck art (AC121 stays).
    3. **Carousel:** the player's own saved decks only (**never starter decks**) as Deck_image.jpg pictures (2× their old size) with name plates; each is a button whose accessible name starts with the deck name (e.g. "Alpha, 1 card"); the centered deck is the one being edited and has **`aria-current="true"`**; a final slot button **"+ New Deck"** is always present (so the carousel is never empty; it does the same as "New Deck"). Arrow buttons **"Previous deck"** and **"Next deck"**; with a carousel deck focused, ArrowLeft/ArrowRight move one deck; clicking a side deck centers it; grabbing with the mouse (pointer events) or a finger and dragging right brings earlier decks to the center (a drag of 120 px or half a deck width, whichever is smaller, moves at least one deck), snapping on release.
    4. The selected deck's **name** (`data-part="deck-name"`, text exactly the deck name), bright gold, ≥ 32 px at 1920×1080, the most prominent text after the title; under it the count line (`data-part="deck-size"`, e.g. "62 cards (50–100)") at most half the name's font size.
    5. One row of text actions **"Auto-fill"**, **"Save"**, **"Rename"**, **"New Deck"** (in that order, tops within 8 px), and directly under them **"Back"** (font at least as big as the actions; the only Back on this screen), above the deck list. R5a.3 style.
    6. The **deck list** (`aria-label="Deck list"`, as before) with a **"Deck list" heading** (≥ 32 px at 1920×1080), "Deck counts", "Deck problems", "Cost chart" (taller bars, numbers ≥ 4× old size) and one **`<li>` per card** ("3 × Snotskull Grunt" + "Add …"/"Remove …"), each row ≥ 40 px tall with entry text ≥ 20 px at 1920×1080. The list scrolls inside its panel (gold scrollbar).
  - **Space priority:** when space is short the deck list gets first claim on height: at least **8 rows fully visible at 1280×720** and **at least 7 at 844×390** (with 12+ distinct cards); title, logo, carousel and name shrink together to make room.
  - **Dragging:** pointer-based drag (not native HTML5 drag images) with a floating DOM copy of the card marked **`data-drag-ghost`**, shown for the whole drag at the width a grid card has at **3 per row** (±10 %), whatever the slider says. Dropping on the Deck pile or the Deck list adds one copy; the ghost is removed after the drop.
- **R5a.6 Starter decks in the deck picker** ("Deck choice" on the select screen). Under each deck stack (siblings, never nested inside the stack button) two R5a.3 text actions: **"Remove"** (accessible name **"Remove <Deck name>"**) and, for starter decks, **"Copy to my decks"** (accessible name **"Copy <Deck name> to my decks"**). Saved decks get "Remove" too.
  - Remove opens an in-game floating dialog **"Remove deck?"** (role `dialog`, mentions the deck name) with **"Remove"** and **"Cancel"**; never a browser `confirm()`. A removed starter deck is hidden for this player (saved in localStorage, survives reload); a removed saved deck is deleted. **At least one deck must remain:** when only one deck is left its Remove is disabled (`disabled` or `aria-disabled="true"`).
  - **"Restore starter decks"** (R5a.3 text button) on the select screen and in Settings brings back all nine starter decks.
  - Copy to my decks saves a copy at once named **"<Deck name> (copy)"** (e.g. "Goblin Warband (copy)", same 100 cards, renameable) and opens the Deck Builder with it centered in the carousel; the starter deck is unchanged. If 5 decks are already saved, nothing is copied and "You can save up to 5 decks." is shown.
- **R5a.7 Choose Your Commander** (select screen): Commander cards 2× their old size (each ≥ 30 % of the window height at 1280×720 and 844×390, fully on-screen), the three spread across at least 45 % of the width; each has a bright rim glow / backlight (`box-shadow` or `drop-shadow` filter on the card or inside it), the selected one glows strongest (a different, stronger glow); the wall behind the card row is dimmed and blurred (a `backdrop-filter`/`filter` with `blur(`). Deck stacks 2× (each ≥ 15 % of the window height). All text 2× (the "Choose Your Commander" heading ≥ 28 px at 1280×720). The layout spreads to fill the screen (R5a.4) with Fight! on-screen and no page scroll.
- **R5a.8 Real spell frames.** Copy the owner's files from `factory-design/design/references/card-frames./` (folder name ends with a dot): `spell-frame-goblin.jpg` (Goblin spells), `spell-frame-gnome.jpg` (Gnome), `spell-frame-dwarf.jpg` (Dwarf), `spell-frame-shared.jpg` (shared) into the game, replacing the round 4 stand-ins (served as `/art/frames/spell-frame-<x>.jpg` or `.webp`, full resolution 1152 × 1712, same proportions). Line the cost (top-left) and spell type (bottom-right) up exactly with the painted orbs (AC132 positions still hold).
- **R5a.9 Fixed checks:** AC72 expects the nine starter stacks as "…, 100 cards, ready"; AC74 expects the Dwarf Warband's 100 cards after Play again; AC130 forces its double-click on the `aria-disabled` locked tile (Playwright refuses to click disabled elements otherwise). AC86 now expects "Alegreya SC" in CREDITS.md (not Cinzel/Nunito). All "Back" lookups are exact.

**Report:** include screenshots at 1920 × 1080 and 844 × 390 of the Deck Builder, Choose Your Commander and one spell card per faction (AC148 saves them as test outputs).

### Phase 1B, Round 4 (spells, starter decks, lettering) — done

Source: the "Round 4" section at the top of `factory-design/design/oreheart-phase-1b-brief.md` and the updated GDD (faction spell table, 100-card starter decks, faction personalities). Both are binding; read them in full. Keep everything from Rounds 3a and 3b. **Not in this round:** Build B (Meshy 3D models). Where this section conflicts with older text (R3.11 deck sizes, R3.12 "50 cards" stack names, R7.9 spell names, the Look & feel fonts, R2.1 frames for spells), **this section wins**. New criteria: AC127–AC134. Older criteria updated: AC6, AC91, AC112, AC117 and the R3.12 stack names. AC123 is fixed.

- **R4.1 Mulligan cards at double size.** The faces in the "Mulligan" dialog are twice their round 3b size, still in **one straight row with no overlap, fully on-screen**. On a phone the gaps shrink first, then the cards scale to fit. Testable: at 1280×720 each face (the element with `data-card-id`) is **at least 300 px tall**; at 844×390 each face is **at least 165 px tall**; in both, the five faces sit in one row (tops within 2 px), don't overlap, and are inside the viewport.
- **R4.2 Nine 100-card starter decks** (replaces R3.11's mix; ids, names, `style` and the picker order stay). Each deck has exactly **100 cards**: **30** of the faction's basic Miner (`goblin-scrounger` / `gnome-prospector` / `dwarf-miner`) + **20** of its Smith Miner (`goblin-smith-miner` Scrapsmith / `gnome-smith-miner` Arcsmith / `dwarf-smith-miner` Anvilsworn), plus by style:
  - **Warband:** 38 Fighters, 7 spells (at least 1 counterspell), 5 Crystal cards.
  - **Arcane Hoard:** 18 Fighters, 24 spells (at least 2 counterspells), 8 Crystal cards.
  - **Horde:** 46 Fighters, 0 spells, 4 Crystal cards.
  - "Fighter" = a creature with role `fighter`. The decks hold no other Miners. Spells are only the deck faction's own spells plus the shared ones. Every deck is legal (`validateDeck(cards, <faction's Commander>)` is legal): copy limits (Rare/Ultra Rare/Collector at most 3), at most 20 Crystal cards, no disabled cards. Fighters are mostly that faction's Commons with plenty of cost ≤ 4; each Warband still has at least one copy of each of its faction's five Revision 8 fighters (AC6).
  - `listStarterDecks()` returns `cards` of length 100. The deck picker stacks are named **"<Deck name>, 100 cards, ready"** (e.g. "Goblin Warband, 100 cards, ready"); saved decks keep their own count.
- **R4.3 Faction spells** (engine, content, AI, Deck Builder, tests). Exact list from the GDD spell table. Renamed spells **keep their internal ids**. `getCard(id).faction` is the spell's faction (`"goblins"`, `"gnomes"`, `"dwarves"`) or **`null` for shared spells**:

  | id | Name | Faction | Rarity | Speed | Cost | Effect |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | `venom-shiv` (new) | Venom Shiv | goblins | uncommon | regular | 2 | Poison an enemy creature (below) |
  | `tunnel-rush` | Rat-Tunnel Ambush | goblins | uncommon | regular | 2 | (old Burrow Ambush effect) |
  | `hold-it` | Pickpocket! | goblins | rare | counter | 4 | (old Not Today! effect) |
  | `fireball` | Searing Arcane Orb | gnomes | rare | regular | 3 | 3 damage to one creature or the enemy Commander |
  | `meteor-strike` | Starfall Cataclysm | gnomes | ultra-rare | regular | 7 | 2 damage to every creature in a 3×3 area |
  | `spell-snap` | Unweave | gnomes | rare | counter | 5 | Cancel any enemy spell |
  | `shield-bubble` | Ironclad Bulwark | dwarves | uncommon | instant | 2 | (old Bastion Ward effect) |
  | `battle-cry` | Thunderhorn Blast | dwarves | rare | regular | 4 | (old Warhowl effect) |
  | `second-wind` | Emergency Repairs | dwarves | ultra-rare | instant | 5 | Your Commander heals 4 |
  | `healing-light` | Field Bandage | shared | common | instant | 2 | (old Mending Light effect) |
  | `fizzle` | Snuff Out | shared | uncommon | counter | 3 | Cancel an enemy spell costing 3 or less |
  | `prospectors-prayer` | Desperate Plunder | shared | common | regular | 1 | (unchanged) |

  - Every on-screen text (cards, callouts such as **"SPROINGLE CASTS SEARING ARCANE ORB!"**, Respond buttons such as **"Ironclad Bulwark"**, **"Soul Cluster + Snuff Out"**, Rules page, tutorial) uses the new names.
  - **Casting lock:** a faction spell can only be cast (Regular, Instant or counterspell, including in the Respond window) when the caster's Commander belongs to that faction. `legalActions()` never lists such a cast, and `act()` returns `{ ok: false, error }` for it. A locked counterspell never opens the Respond window. The AI obeys the lock.
  - **Test-mode switch `factionLock`** (new `newMatch` option, test mode only, default **`true`**): `false` turns the casting lock off so older setup-based tests can put any spell in any hand. Normal play always has the lock on.
  - **Deck validation:** `validateDeck(cardIds, commanderId?)`. When `commanderId` is given, every faction spell of another faction adds the problem **"<Spell name>: only for <Faction> Commanders."** (Faction = Goblin, Gnome or Dwarf; e.g. "Searing Arcane Orb: only for Gnome Commanders."). Without `commanderId` this check is skipped. A disabled card adds **"<Name> is not available yet."**
  - **Deck Builder:** a custom dropdown (`role="combobox"`, R3b.6 style) named **"Deck Commander"** with options **"Grubnak"**, **"Sproingle"**, **"Borgo"** (default Grubnak for a new deck; saved with the deck; older saves default to Grubnak). Other factions' spell tiles stay in the grid but are **locked**: `aria-disabled="true"`, dimmed, with a 🔒 and the hint "Only for <Faction> Commanders"; double-click, drag and + do nothing. Spells of another faction already in the deck show the problem above in "Deck problems". The select screen marks a saved deck "not ready" for a Commander that can't use all its spells (same message when tapped).
  - **Venom Shiv poison:** target = an enemy creature. On cast nothing is dealt. The creature gets `poison` = 3 in `getState()` (every creature has `poison`, 0 when not poisoned). At the **start of each of its owner's turns** while `poison > 0`: it takes **1 damage**, `poison` drops by 1, and it does **not** heal to full that turn; while poisoned no heal of any kind restores Health (Field Bandage can't target it). Exact example: an enemy creature at full Health H ≥ 4 is poisoned on your turn → after the enemy's next turn starts it has H−1 (poison 2), still H−1 during your next turn, H−2 (poison 1) at the enemy's following turn, H−3 (poison 0) at the turn after that, and it heals back to full H at the start of the enemy's turn after that. Poison damage can defeat the creature (normal defeat effects). UI: a green poison drop marker with the turns left on the creature, `aria-label` **"Poisoned, N turns left"** in its stat pill, plus green bubbles on the figure. fx log: the cast logs `effect` with the spell's `visual`, and each tick logs `{ kind: "poison", uid, amount: 1 }`.
  - **Hex Reversal** (`mirror-hex`) is removed from play: `getCard("mirror-hex")` still returns it with **`disabled: true`** (every other card has `disabled: false`); `listCards()` **omits** disabled cards; it is not in the Deck Builder, any starter deck or the AI's choices; `validateDeck` rejects it.
  - Each spell keeps a unique `visual` (R3b.7, AC124) matching the GDD's "Spell effects" descriptions (Venom Shiv: a dagger flies in, the target turns sickly green with dripping bubbles; Rat-Tunnel Ambush: rats burrow, the creature pops out of a hole; Pickpocket!: a goblin hand snatches the card away; and so on).
- **R4.4 Spell card frames** (owner art). Expected files `factory-design/design/references/card-frames/spell-frame-goblin.*` (green filigree, purple glow), `spell-frame-gnome.*` (gold with blue), `spell-frame-dwarf.*` (lavender silver with purple), `spell-frame-shared.*` (silver with rainbow). **They are not uploaded yet:** build close temporary copies (e.g. recolored from the creature frames with the two round orbs painted in) served as `/art/frames/spell-frame-<goblin|gnome|dwarf|shared>.webp`, so swapping in the real files is a one-line change (one config entry or just replacing the files). Every spell face (hand, reveal, card view, Deck Builder, mulligan, Respond) uses its frame: the face's `data-frame` is **`spell-goblin`**, **`spell-gnome`**, **`spell-dwarf`** or **`spell-shared`** and it contains an `<img>` whose `src` contains `spell-frame-<x>` (and no `card-frame-` img). Layout: full-art spell painting filling the frame window; **cost** as the 3D gold number in the round orb at the **top-left corner** (the `aria-label="Cost N"` element's center in the left 30 % and top 25 % of the face); **spell type** in the round orb at the **bottom-right corner** (the `aria-label="Type: Spell"` or `"Type: Instant"` element's center in the right 30 % and bottom 30 % of the face): a **wand** + "SPELL" for Regular spells, a **lightning bolt** + "INSTANT" for Instants and counterspells (the element's text contains SPELL or INSTANT); name and short text at the bottom over a soft dark fade. No Attack/Health/Movement. The rarity crystal and "Rarity: <Word>" element (R3b.2) stay. Creature and Crystal cards keep their R2.1 frames.
- **R4.5 (superseded by R5a.1) Legible metal lettering everywhere** (replaces the Look & feel fonts and R3b's gold-text styling methods). Two highly legible free fonts for commercial use, via Google Fonts or self-hosted under `public/assets/external/`, listed in `CREDITS.md`: **"Cinzel"** (weight 700, the bold classic carved serif) for stat numbers, card names, titles, headings and buttons, and **"Tinos"** (the Times New Roman-style serif) for card text, menus, rules, settings and all other text. Generate two photorealistic seamless metal textures with the image API (no text): **`/art/ui/metal-gold.webp`** (polished gold) and **`/art/ui/metal-silver.webp`** (polished silver). Letters are filled with them (`background-image` with the texture and/or a metal gradient, `background-clip: text` / `-webkit-background-clip: text`, transparent text fill) with bevel, highlight, thin dark outline and soft shadow so they read on any background: four sets, gold/silver × Cinzel/Tinos. Small text (under 18 px) uses a cleaner, brighter version (subtle gradient, little texture) with generous size and line spacing (card text ≥ 11 px on an 844×390 hand card, line-height ≥ 1.2); the full photoreal texture only on large numbers and titles. Gold is the default; silver for secondary text (flavor lines) and wherever gold would blend into a gold frame. **Testable rule:** on the title screen, Settings, Rules, Deck Builder, select screen and match screen, every visible text node containing a letter or digit (ignoring `aria-hidden` subtrees, `<canvas>`/`<svg>` content and the values typed inside text boxes) has an ancestor element with **`data-metal="gold"`** or **`data-metal="silver"`** whose computed `font-family` starts with **Cinzel** or **Tinos** and whose computed `background-clip` or `-webkit-background-clip` is `text` with a `background-image` other than `none`. At least one element on the title screen uses `metal-gold` in its `background-image`. The 3D canvas callouts (e.g. "KA-BOOM!", spell callouts) use the same fonts and metal look where practical (not tested).
- **R4.6 Fix the flawed check AC123** (its "Back" lookup also matched card names like "Backstab Weasel"; it now uses an exact match) and confirm the welcome message position and the `data-ornament="frame"` ornaments on the **Deck Builder, Rules and Settings** screens (AC123 now also checks Rules and Settings).

**Report:** include phone-size (844 × 390) screenshots of the mulligan screen, a spell card per faction (goblin, gnome, dwarf, shared), a creature card close-up and a menu screen (AC134 saves them as test outputs).

### Phase 1B, Round 3b (visual polish) — done

Source: the "Round 3b" section at the top of `factory-design/design/oreheart-phase-1b-brief.md` (binding, read it in full). **Rules are unchanged.** Keep everything from Round 3a. **Not in this round:** Build B (Meshy 3D models, deck-builder 3D viewer). Where this section conflicts with older text (R2.1's "card text in the frame's faction color", R2.20 / R9.1's "filters stay native `<select>`s"), **this section wins**. New criteria: AC118–AC126; updated older criteria are listed under "Round 3b notes on older criteria".

- **R3b.1 Card numbers** (cost, Attack, Health, Movement) on every card face everywhere: smaller, each fully inside its frame slot and precisely centered in the circle, shield, heart and the notch under the boot, in **3D metallic gold** matching the frame gold (gold gradient or gold color, bevel/highlight, thin dark outline via `text-shadow`, `-webkit-text-stroke` or `drop-shadow`). Testable form: the element with `aria-label` "Cost N" / "Attack N" / "Health N" / "Movement N" (R2.1) **is the whole slot area** (the circle, shield, heart or notch) and contains the number as DOM text; the number's text box lies inside the slot, its center within 20 % of the slot's width/height of the slot's center; its font size is at most 10 % of the card face height; gold (a `background-image` gradient clipped to text with transparent fill, or a gold color: red ≥ 150, green ≥ 100, red ≥ green, green − blue ≥ 30) with an outline (any of the three techniques above).
- **R3b.2 Rarity crystal** (bottom center): a fancy faceted art crystal like the mine crystal nodes, drawn as an inline `<svg>` or `<img>` inside the "Rarity: <Word>" element (R2.1), **smaller** than before (3–12 % of the face height), centered horizontally on the card (within 8 % of the face width), in the lowest quarter of the card, glowing in the rarity color (Common white, Uncommon blue, Rare purple, Ultra Rare red, Collector rainbow metallic gold; the AC91 color checks on the rarity element stay). The rarity word is smaller, as DOM text inside the same element, **centered just below the crystal** (its top at or below the crystal's middle, horizontal centers within 6 % of the face width).
- **R3b.3 Card names:** element `data-part="name"` on every card face; 3D gold in the OREHEART logo lettering style with a thin dark outline; **one uniform font size** on every card (all names of 16 characters or fewer share exactly the same computed size at the same card size); only very long names shrink, never below 60 % of that size, and never overflow (no ellipsis or clipping).
- **R3b.4 Text box:** element `data-part="text"` on every card face holding the short text (R2.2) and flavor line; **one universal font size** on every card (identical computed size at the same card size; never truncated), **thinner, not bold** (font-weight ≤ 500), stylized gold with a thin dark outline. **All card text is now gold** (replaces R2.1's faction-colored text). Tapping the text still shows "Full explanation" (R2.2).
- **R3b.5 Deck Builder polish:** the deck artwork (`deck-image`, R2.3) in "Deck pile" floats with **no border, box or frame**: the image and every ancestor up to the "Deck pile" element have no border, outline, background color/image or `box-shadow` (a soft glow uses `filter: drop-shadow`, which follows the cut-out). The name plate under the stack stays. The Deck Builder background becomes a rich painted scene in the game's style (e.g. a dark war room or forge cavern lit by glowing crystals), generated with the image API, **no text**, file path containing **`deck-builder-bg`** (e.g. `/art/ui/deck-builder-bg.webp`), used as an `<img>` or CSS `background-image`.
- **R3b.6 One ornate control style everywhere:** every button, slider ("Cards per row", volumes: gold filigree track with a gem/crest handle), switch/toggle, tab, dropdown, search bar, checkbox, + / − stepper and scrollbar shares the same intricate gold filigree, gem accents, glow and press animation. Testable rules on every screen (title, Settings, Deck Builder, Commander and deck select, match):
  - Every visible native `button`, `input`, `textarea` has computed **`appearance: none`** (e.g. a global `button, input, textarea { appearance: none; }` plus the ornate styling). Native inputs hidden behind a custom control (opacity 0, `visibility: hidden` or ≤ 2 px) are allowed.
  - **No visible native `<select>`.** The Deck Builder filters **Faction, Type, Cost, Rarity** (exactly the options and behavior of R8.2: Faction "All", "Goblins", "Gnomes", "Dwarves", "Neutral"; Type "All", "Miners", "Fighters", "Spells", "Crystal cards"; Cost "Any", "1-2", "3-4", "5-6", "7+"; Rarity "All", "Common", "Uncommon", "Rare", "Ultra Rare", "Collector") become custom dropdowns: a `role="combobox"` element named exactly by the filter label and showing the current choice as text; clicking it opens a floating `role="listbox"` with the same accessible name (no box: floating gold lettering) of `role="option"` items named exactly by the option texts; clicking an option selects it and closes the list; Escape closes it. Any other dropdown follows the same pattern.
  - **Scrollbars:** every scrollable element (and the page itself if it scrolls) shows an ornate gold scrollbar: computed `scrollbar-color` is set (not `auto`) with a gold thumb (first color: red ≥ 120, red ≥ green > blue) on a thin dark-gold track (e.g. `* { scrollbar-color: #c9a24a #2a1d0c; scrollbar-width: thin; }`, plus matching `::-webkit-scrollbar` filigree styling for the thumb art). Elements that deliberately hide their scrollbar (`scrollbar-width: none`, e.g. the hand fan with its arrows) are allowed. Never the white/grey browser scrollbar.
- **R3b.7 Build A leftovers:**
  - **Ornaments:** finish the gold filigree ornaments framing every screen (title, select, mulligan, match, Deck Builder, Rules, Settings): each screen renders at least one decorative element with **`data-ornament="frame"`** (`aria-hidden="true"`, `pointer-events: none`). Finish the title layout (close to `menu screen.jpg`).
  - **Welcome text fix:** the "Welcome to Oreheart!" dialog (5.2) never overlaps the logo or the **Tutorial** button, at 844×390 and at 1280×720: none of its text (heading or message, excluding its own buttons) intersects the h1 logo image or the menu's Tutorial button (e.g. place it below/beside the menu, or move the menu aside while it shows).
  - **Unique spell visuals:** every spell gets its own step-by-step visual (card pops up, turns into the effect, the result lands) instead of reused old effects. `getCard(id)` gains **`visual`**: for spells, a non-empty string id unique among all spells (never "generic", "default" or "none"); `null` for other cards. The fx log `effect` entry of a spell (R2.16) carries `visual` equal to that id, and the order stays `reveal` (when Card pop-ups is on) → `effect` → `impact`.
  - **Tutorial text rewrite** for everything added since Phase 1A (same steps as R9.10, same keywords; additionally): the `summon` tip mentions your Stronghold in the bottom-left **"corner"**; the `move` tip mentions that you can **"zoom"** (pinch / wheel) and pan the battlefield and tap a creature to see its card; the `spell` tip mentions tapping card text for the **"Full explanation"**; the `savings` tip mentions the **"Last turn"** button to see what the enemy did. No tip mentions "column 7", "top of the screen" or "bottom center".
- **R3b.8 Updated round 3a tests:** AC9 example (b) and AC27 summoning costs now summon onto free back-row spaces (columns 1, 3, 4, 5 …); nothing may be placed on a Stronghold space (unchanged).

**Report:** include phone-size (844 × 390) screenshots of several cards up close (one per faction and one per rarity), the Deck Builder, the title screen and a spell being cast (AC126 saves them as test outputs).

### Phase 1B, Round 3a (board and rules) — done

Source: the "Round 3a" section at the top of `factory-design/design/oreheart-phase-1b-brief.md` (binding, read it in full) and the updated GDD (corner Strongholds, 50–100 card decks, up to 20 Crystal cards, nine starter decks). **Not in this round:** round 3b visual polish and Build B (Meshy 3D models). Everything else from Build A (Round 2) stays. Where this section conflicts with anything older in this spec (R2.10's diagonal camera, R2.11/R2.12 enemy-hand position, R5.1/R5.2 Stronghold columns and mirroring, R8.2/R8.3/R8.4/R9.1/R9.4 deck size, Crystal limit and "Starter deck"), **this section wins**. New criteria: AC109–AC117; updated older criteria are listed under "Round 3a notes on older criteria".

- **R3.1 Camera: straight-on road perspective.** Undo R2.10's diagonal. The board faces the player straight on and fills the whole stage (the "Battlefield" element covers the viewport in Auto mode). Rows are level on screen (all creatures of one row have label centers within 12 px vertically); near rows are lower and wider, far rows higher and narrower, like looking down a road (at 844×390 the on-screen distance between columns 3 and 11 on row 7 is more than 1.1× that on row 1, and the columns converge toward the far side). Zoom and pan (R2.19) stay.
- **R3.2 Strongholds in the corners (engine rule).** The player's Stronghold + Commander is on **row 7, column 2** (column 1 is a normal, empty space beside it); the enemy's on **row 1, column 12** (column 13 normal). Nothing can stand on or be summoned to a Stronghold space; all 8 spaces around each Stronghold are normal spaces. Back rows (1 and 7) therefore have 12 summonable spaces each: row 7 = columns 1 and 3–13, row 1 = columns 1–11 and 13. "Adjacent to the enemy Stronghold" (melee attacks on the Commander) and Ranged distance 2 are measured from these new spaces. State gets **`strongholds: { player: { row: 7, col: 2 }, opponent: { row: 1, col: 12 } }`**. Config: `playerStronghold: { row: 7, col: 2 }`, `opponentStronghold: { row: 1, col: 12 }`.
- **R3.3 Corner-mirrored fair mines.** Random layouts (seeded) mirror **corner-to-corner**: a mine at (r, c) always has a twin at (8 − r, 14 − c). Either one mine at the exact center (4, 7) plus a pair (3, c) / (5, 14 − c), or all three on row 4: (4, 7) plus (4, c) / (4, 14 − c). Still: rows 3–5 only, at least 2 apart (Chebyshev), never adjacent to a Stronghold (Chebyshev ≥ 2 from (7,2) and (1,12)), listed in reading order. The `newMatch` option `mines` (test only) accepts any 3 distinct spaces in rows 3–5 that are not adjacent to a Stronghold and does **not** have to be mirrored (the tests' fixed layout (4,2), (4,4), (4,6) stays valid). The tutorial's fixed layout is redrawn for the new Strongholds (any fixed corner-mirrored layout), and its scripted steps, highlights and text are updated so it still completes.
- **R3.4 AI and tutorial updated:** the computer opponent's movement, mining and attack planning target the new Stronghold corners (it marches toward (7,2) and defends (1,12)); the tutorial still teaches every step and `tutorialAction()` still completes it.
- **R3.5 Oversized Commanders and Strongholds:** drawn noticeably larger (exaggerated, ~1.5–2× a space) but still occupying exactly one space; creatures stand on all spaces around them and remain tappable (spread spaces slightly if needed).
- **R3.6 Hands around the Commanders (844×390):** the player's hand fan ("Your hand") sits along the bottom shifted **right** (its center in the right half), clear of the player's Commander at the bottom-left; "Your deck" at the bottom-right (center x > 2/3 width, lower half). The enemy card backs ("Enemy hand", the element containing the `data-card-back` elements) sit along the top shifted **left** (center in the left half, upper half), clear of the enemy Commander at the top-right; "Enemy deck" at the top-left (center x < 1/3 width, upper half). **Every enemy card back is always fully on-screen and visible** (at least 20 px tall), including while it lifts when the AI browses. The enemy-hand card count stays inside or beside "Enemy hand".
- **R3.7 Hand width cap:** the player's fan has a fixed comfortable width (at most 70 % of the stage width, never stretched) and shows about **7** cards (5–8 visible at once); with more cards the player swipes/scrolls/drags through it, with buttons **"Scroll hand left"** and **"Scroll hand right"** (shown when there are more cards than fit; "Scroll hand left" appears once scrolled) plus a fade at the ends, and a count element `aria-label="Hand count"` whose text contains the number of cards in hand. Every hand card is still a button named by its card name containing its face (`data-card-id`), even when scrolled out of view. The enemy fan follows the same cap, smaller.
- **R3.8 Creature card pop-up size:** the creature card shown by tapping (R2.14 dialog) or hovering a creature is the **same size as a hovered hand card** (about 2× a resting hand card: face height between 1.6× and 2.5× a hand card button's height) and fully on-screen.
- **R3.9 HUD around the corners:** the player's counters ("Your Flow crystals", "Your Bank crystals", "Your Commander health", "Last turn") beside/above the player's Commander without covering it ("Your Commander health" center x < 40 % width, lower half); the enemy's beside theirs ("Enemy Commander health" center x > 60 % width, upper half). Far-row tap/drop targets are slightly larger than they look. "Current phase", "Done", "Card pop-ups" and "Re-center" stay as in R2.11.
- **R3.10 Deck size 50–100, up to 20 Crystal cards.** Config `deckMin: 50`, `deckMax: 100`, `crystalCardLimit: 20` (all deck checks read them). Messages (Deck Builder "Deck problems", select-screen "Deck message", `validateDeck`): **"Needs 50 to 100 cards (now N)."** (fewer than 50 or more than 100), **"At most 20 Crystal cards (now N)."**, plus the unchanged copy-limit and Unique messages. Deck Builder counts text: **"Cards: N (50–100) · Creatures: N · Spells: N · Crystal cards: N"**; the "Deck pile" shows **"N cards (50–100)"** (en dash; replaces "N/50"). Adding past 100 is allowed but flagged. **Auto-fill** completes a deck below 50 up to exactly 50 (Miners, cost curve, a few spells, Crystal cards up to 4); a deck of 50+ is left as is.
- **R3.11 Nine starter decks** in `starter-decks.json`, all 50 cards and legal: ids `<f>-warband`, `<f>-arcane-hoard`, `<f>-horde` for f = `goblin`, `gnome`, `dwarf`; names exactly **"<F> Warband"**, **"<F> Arcane Hoard"**, **"<F> Horde"** with F = Goblin, Gnome, Dwarf. Mostly that faction's cards, mostly Commons, at most 4 copies of any card other than the Miners, within all copy limits:
  - **Warband:** 40 creatures (7–9 Miners: exactly 5 basic + 3 Smith of the faction, as before), 6 spells (including at least 1 Snuff Out, id `fizzle`), 4 Crystal cards. (The old single starter deck becomes the Warband; AC6 still holds for it.)
  - **Arcane Hoard:** 26 creatures (10–14 Miners), 16 spells (at least 2 counterspells), 8 Crystal cards.
  - **Horde:** 46 creatures (12–16 Miners), 0 spells, 4 Crystal cards.
  - Miners = creatures with role `gatherer` or `smith`.
- **R3.12 Deck picker (R9.4 "Deck choice" row):** no "Starter deck" stack any more. The row shows the chosen Commander's faction's three starter decks first (Warband, Arcane Hoard, Horde), then the other six starter decks (Goblin, Gnome, Dwarf order, skipping the chosen faction), then saved decks in save order, then "+ New Deck". Each starter stack is a button named **"<Deck name>, 50 cards, ready"** with the deck image (`img` src containing `deck-image`) and its name plate. Default and R9.8 fallback selection: the chosen Commander's faction's Warband; if the selected deck is a starter deck and the Commander changes, the selection switches to the new faction's Warband. The selected stack has `aria-pressed="true"`.
- **R3.13 Computer's deck:** in normal play the AI picks one of its Commander's faction's three starter decks with the match's seeded RNG. In test mode the opponent's default is its faction's Warband (for determinism) unless `opponentDeckId` is given. The AI must be able to play every style (including a spell-heavy Arcane Hoard and a spell-less Horde) to the end of a match without stalling.
- **R3.14 Fix the step-by-step spell check (AC100):** the test now respects Commander bonuses (e.g. Thane Borgo's +1 Health): Infernal Orb deals exactly 3; if the target survives, a second Orb defeats it, and the fx log shows effect → impact → damage → defeat. Engine rules are unchanged.

**New and changed test hooks (Round 3a):**
- `newMatch` options: **`playerDeckId`**, **`opponentDeckId`** (a starter deck id from R3.11; default: the player's faction Warband, and in test mode the opponent's faction Warband); `setup.playerDeck`/`opponentDeck` still override. **`aiBoth: true`** = the computer plays both sides (test mode only; AI acts immediately, mulligans automatic when `skipMulligan` is not set) until the match ends.
- State: `strongholds` (R3.2); `players[side].deckId` = the starter deck id used, or `null` for a saved or `setup` deck.
- **`listStarterDecks()`** → array of 9 `{ id, name, faction: "goblins"|"gnomes"|"dwarves", style: "warband"|"arcane-hoard"|"horde", cards: [cardId × 50] }`.
- **`validateDeck(cardIds)`** → `{ legal: boolean, problems: string[] }` using exactly the R3.10 / R8.2 messages (empty `problems` when legal).
- `getCard` additionally returns **`speed`**: `"regular"` | `"instant"` | `"counter"` for spells (counterspells are `"counter"`), `null` otherwise.
- `getConfig()` includes `deckMin`, `deckMax`, `crystalCardLimit`, `playerStronghold`, `opponentStronghold`.

**Report:** include phone-size (844 × 390) screenshots of a match in progress with both hands visible and of the deck picker (AC115 and AC117 also save them as test outputs).

### Phase 1B, Round 2 (Build A)

Source: the "Round 2 (Build A)" section at the top of `factory-design/design/oreheart-phase-1b-brief.md` (binding, read it in full) and the updated GDD (short card text, 3 mulligans, Respond only when playable). New owner art (binding, use the files themselves, scaled and compressed): `factory-design/design/references/card-frames/card-frame-goblin.jpg` (green), `card-frame-gnome.jpg` (blue), `card-frame-dwarf.jpg` (purple), and `references/Deck_image.jpg` (the deck image; its grey-and-white checkerboard is fake transparency and must be cut out to real alpha). **Not in this round:** new 3D creature models and the deck-builder 3D viewer (Build B); keep the current figures working. Everything not changed here stays as in Phase 1A and Round 1, and all older acceptance tests keep passing except the ones listed under "Round 2 notes on older criteria" (AC44, AC49, AC51, AC57), which are updated. New criteria: AC91–AC108.

- **R2.1 Card layout on the owner's frames** (every non-Commander card face: hand, reveals, card views, Deck Builder, mulligan, Respond dialog). The frame is an `<img>` whose `src` contains `card-frame-goblin`, `card-frame-gnome` or `card-frame-dwarf` by the card's faction (served from `/art/frames/`, e.g. `/art/frames/card-frame-goblin.webp`); **neutral** cards use a grey-silver recolor saved as `card-frame-neutral`. The face element (the one with `data-card-id`, 7.8) also gets `data-frame` = `goblin` | `gnome` | `dwarf` | `neutral`. Content drawn by the game on top: top-left circle **cost** (`aria-label="Cost N"`), top-right shield **Attack** (`aria-label="Attack N"`), heart under it **Health** (`aria-label="Health N"`), bottom-left boot **Movement** in the notch (`aria-label="Movement N"`), bottom-right circle **type icon** (`aria-label="Type: <T>"`, T one of Miner, Fighter, Ranged, Bomb, Guard, Spell, Instant, Crystal; the painted pickaxe in the frame is covered), the picture in the big window, the name on the middle bar, the **short text** and flavor line in the lower box, and the **rarity gem** at bottom-center glowing in the rarity color with the rarity word beside it (element `aria-label="Rarity: <Word>"`, text exactly the word: Common, Uncommon, Rare, Ultra Rare, Collector). Spells and Crystal cards have **no** Attack, Health or Movement elements. One font for all card text, colored in the frame's faction color (green, blue, purple; neutral silver) with a dark outline/glow. **Rarity colors** (computed CSS `color` of the rarity word): Common white (each RGB channel ≥ 220), Uncommon blue (blue channel highest, ≥ 40 above red), Rare purple (red and blue both ≥ 40 above green), Ultra Rare red (red ≥ 60 above green and blue), Collector rainbow metallic gold (gold base with a slowly moving rainbow shimmer: a CSS animation is running, `animation-name` not `none`). The 7.8 Round-1 frame description is replaced by this one; `data-card-id`, `data-rarity` and the picture `<img>` stay.
- **R2.2 Short card text + tap for more.** Every card in `cards.json` gets `shortText` (plain, understandable on its own, at most 80 characters, e.g. "Ranged. Enemies can't strike back.") and `longText` (full rules: keywords explained, when it can be played, what it counts as, limits, and a line starting **"Example:"**; longer than `shortText`). `getCard` returns both; **`text` now equals `shortText`** (so older checks that look for `text` on faces keep working). Faces show `shortText`, never truncated. In every enlarged card view (the hand-card view dialog, the Deck Builder enlarged view, the creature card view R2.14, a pinned reveal R2.13) the short-text area is a button with accessible name **"Full explanation"**; tapping it floats the long text over the screen as `role="dialog"` named **"<Card Name>: full explanation"** containing `longText`; tapping it again (or its **"Close"** button) closes it. Keyword wording moves into `longText`: Ironwall Shieldbeard's contains "Guard: adjacent enemies must attack it first"; Smith Miners' contain "+1 Attack and +1 Health"; basic Miners' contain "+1 Health"; Ranged cards' contain "Ranged" (and their `shortText` too).
- **R2.3 Deck image everywhere.** `Deck_image.jpg` is cut out to a transparent PNG/WebP served at `/art/deck/deck-image.webp` (or `.png`; the path contains `deck-image`); no checkerboard pixel may remain (the image's corners are fully transparent). Every deck pile uses it as an `<img>` whose `src` contains `deck-image`: **"Your deck"** and **"Enemy deck"** in the match (count still shown as text, the stack shrinks with scale), the Deck Builder **"Deck pile"**, and every select-screen deck stack. A deck's name sits on a small ornate gold plate under its stack.
- **R2.4 Commander select:** the three Commander buttons (accessible names unchanged) become **borderless full-art cards**: a portrait `<img>` at `/art/commanders/<commanderId>.webp` (generated with the image API in the reference style; Grubnak as in `menu ref image.jpg`) edge to edge, with a text window at the bottom showing name, title, faction ("Goblins" / "Gnomes" / "Dwarves") and special ability. No cost, Attack, Health or Movement (no elements labelled "Cost …", "Attack …", "Health …", "Movement …" inside). Deck stacks (R2.3), the Easy/Normal/Hard buttons and "Fight!" below as ornate buttons.
- **R2.5 Mulligan (engine + screen).** Each player may mulligan up to **3 times**. Action `{ type: "mulligan", uids }`: `uids: []` keeps the hand and ends that player's mulligan. Non-empty `uids` = mulligan number k (1, 2 or 3): those cards are shuffled back and the player draws `uids.length` cards if k = 1, or `uids.length − 1` if k ≥ 2 (so the hand goes 5 → 5 → 4 → 3 when at least one card is swapped each time). After k < 3 the same player keeps priority in the `mulligan` phase; after the 3rd the player's mulligan ends automatically. `players[side].mulligans` = number used (0–3). A 4th mulligan is illegal. The second player's extra card comes after both players finish (unchanged). The dialog **"Mulligan"** (still `role="dialog"`, no box) shows the hand as real card faces in **one straight row** (no overlap, same vertical position), large; tapping a face marks it (`aria-pressed="true"`); text **"Mulligans left: N"** (3, 2, 1); buttons **"Keep Hand"** and **"Redraw Selected"** (disabled when nothing is marked). After the 3rd redraw the dialog closes and the game continues. The AI mulligans at most once.
- **R2.6 No boxes anywhere.** Menus, pop-ups, prompts, banners, the Respond and Mulligan dialogs, Settings, Rules, the win/lose summary: all float over the board or scene with **no panel, box or background** — every `role="dialog"` element has a computed `background-color` with alpha ≤ 0.35 and `background-image: none` (a soft dark glow behind letters via `text-shadow`/`filter` is fine). Text uses fancy gold display lettering like the OREHEART logo. Turn and phase words ("YOUR TURN", "DEPLOY", "ACTION", "ENEMY TURN") are just these words.
- **R2.7 Ornate polish.** Gold filigree corner pieces and edge flourishes framing every screen (edges and headings only, never behind content). **All buttons** become ornate gold filigree buttons like the PLAY button in `menu screen.jpg` (engraved gold rims, gem caps, icon + fancy lettering, glow on hover/press, press-down animation), drawn with CSS `background-image` or `border-image` (so computed `background-image` or `border-image-source` is not `none`). Icons stay decorative; accessible names unchanged.
- **R2.8 Title screen redo** (close to `menu screen.jpg`): crisp full-quality logo (still the h1 `<img alt="Oreheart">`, no blur, no box, no cut-out edges), the crystal heart glowing and pulsing, embers and crystal sparkles, a painted cavern background **without text** (file path contains `cavern`, e.g. `/art/menu/cavern.webp`, used as an `<img>` or CSS `background-image`) with the Goblin, Gnome and Dwarf heroes and their crystals, gentle parallax and light shimmer, filigree ornaments. Still exactly the five buttons Play, Tutorial, Decks, Rules, Settings (AC1).
- **R2.9 Display mode** on the title screen and in Settings: a `role="radiogroup"` named **"Display mode"** with three radios **"Auto"** (default, fills the screen), **"PC"** (16:9) and **"Phone"** (about 19.5:9, so a PC shows exactly what a phone shows). Radios, not buttons (AC1 stays at five buttons). Saved in localStorage and restored on reload. In PC/Phone mode the game stage (including the **"Battlefield"** element) is the largest centered box of that ratio (width/height 16/9 ± 0.08 or 19.5/9 ± 0.08) inside the window; the leftover sides show decorative art, never plain black bars.
- **R2.10 Diagonal battlefield camera** (like `arena-ref1.jpg`): the board is turned and tilted so the player's Stronghold and Commander are at the **bottom-left** and the enemy's in the **top-right third**, both Commanders clearly visible and large. Same grid, rules and spaces; creatures can still stand next to and surround each Stronghold. On screen, a creature on the player's back row (row 7) appears clearly **left of and below** (at least 60 px left and 60 px lower at 844×390) a creature in the same column on the enemy's back row (row 1). The tucked hand never covers the player's Commander heart. Creature labels/pills (R8.9) still follow their figures.
- **R2.11 Grouped HUD** (no boxes): **player cluster at bottom-left** by the player's Commander: "Your Flow crystals", "Your Bank crystals", "Your Commander health" and the **"Last turn"** button (centers in the left half and the bottom half of the stage). **Enemy cluster at top-right** by the enemy Commander: "Enemy Commander health", enemy crystals and the **"Enemy hand"** count (centers in the right half and the top half). Turn and phase words (`aria-label="Current phase"`) at **top center** (center within the middle third horizontally, upper quarter vertically; replaces R6.8's top-right). **"Done"**, the **"Card pop-ups"** switch and a **"Re-center"** button grouped on the **right edge at mid height** (centers in the right fifth, vertically between 25 % and 75 %). Each player's deck pile next to their own hand ("Your deck" in the bottom half, "Enemy deck" in the top half). "Menu" and "Mute sound" stay in a corner.
- **R2.12 Enemy hand:** the enemy card backs (each an element with the attribute `data-card-back` inside "Enemy hand") fan at the top, **smaller** than the player's hand cards (a back's height ≤ 80 % of a player hand card's height), placed so they don't crowd the enemy Commander; their deck pile clearly visible beside it. While the computer is deciding, its card backs lift one at a time like a hovered card (it "browses"; skipped in test mode).
- **R2.13 Card reveals never slow the game.** A played card (either side) shows in the center (element `aria-label="Card reveal"`, containing the card face) for about 1.5–2 s while the game keeps going (the engine state has already changed; play continues underneath). **Tap it to pin it** (`data-pinned="true"`): it stays until tapped again, then disappears. Replaces R7.5's tap-to-skip. In test mode reveals are instant as before, **unless the URL contains `&fx=1`**, which makes test mode use real reveal and effect timings (for AC97).
- **R2.14 Tap a creature:** tapping/clicking a creature (its figure or its stat pill/label "<Name>, row R, column C") without dragging plays its poke reaction and sound, and its full card floats up out of it to the center as `role="dialog"` named exactly the card's name (with the card face and "Full explanation"); tapping the card or **"Close"** dismisses it. Never changes the game state. With a mouse, hovering a creature for about 0.4 s (not while dragging) shows its card just above it (no dialog). The `poke(uid)` hook still opens nothing (AC85).
- **R2.15 Card pop-ups switch:** `role="switch"` named **"Card pop-ups"** on the match screen (R2.11) and in Settings, `aria-checked="true"` by default, saved in localStorage. Off skips the big card pop-ups (played-card reveals, ability cards, creature cards on tap/hover) but every spell and ability animation still plays.
- **R2.16 Visible spells and abilities, step by step** (both players): (a) the card pops up with its short text (unless pop-ups are off), (b) it turns into the effect (fireball arcs onto the target, meteor falls, shield bubble forms…), (c) the result lands: hit flash, damage numbers, defeat poof. Counterspells: the counter card shows, then the targeted card shatters. **Nothing vanishes from the board without a visible cause.** Comic sound-word starbursts on big hits. Each step short; a tap skips. New hooks (test mode): **`getFxLog()`** → array, in order, of every visual event since the match started: `{ kind, cardId?, uid? }` with kinds `"reveal"` (a big card pop-up was shown; cardId), `"effect"` (a spell/ability animation started; cardId), `"impact"` (an effect landed; cardId), `"damage"` (uid, amount), `"defeat"` (uid; a defeat poof), `"bounce"` (uid; a creature returned to hand), `"shatter"` (cardId of the countered card), `"bombDrop"` (uid), `"boom"` (uid). Every creature that leaves the board gets a `"defeat"` or `"bounce"` entry with its uid. In test mode these are logged even though their durations are zero. **`clearFxLog()`** empties it.
- **R2.17 Exploding creatures** (Blastrat Sapper, Boomfist Lunatic, and any creature whose ability explodes) visibly carry a big bomb prop (`getRenderStats().figures[i].bomb === true`; `false` for others). On death: the bomb drops (`"bombDrop"`), the fuse sparks briefly, then KA-BOOM with the starburst (`"boom"`), then the damage visibly hits adjacent creatures (`"damage"` entries for them), in that order.
- **R2.18 Respond only when playable** (replaces R6.10's opening rule; the GDD wins over the old "always open"): the Respond window (`pendingResponse`) opens only if the responder can actually play a matching Instant or counterspell **right now**: affordable with Flow + Bank, or affordable after playing one Crystal card from hand (when the Crystal limit allows), and legal for this moment. Otherwise there is no window, no pop-up and no wait; the action resolves at once. The dialog lists only playable options (per Instant: a button named the card name if affordable now, else the "<Crystal> + <Instant>" combo), the standalone "Play <Crystal>" buttons (R7.2) and "Let it happen"; no greyed-out "need N more" buttons. Review every other prompt: never stop or ask when there are no real options.
- **R2.19 Battlefield zoom and pan:** pinch (phone) and mouse wheel over the battlefield (PC) zoom between the full-board view (zoom 1) and a close-up (max about 2.5); two-finger drag (phone), right-/middle-button drag or drag on empty ground (PC) pan. One-finger/left drag on a creature or card still moves/plays exactly as before. Double-tap empty ground or the **"Re-center"** button resets to zoom 1 and no pan. Hand, counters and buttons stay fixed. Hook **`getCamera()`** → `{ zoom, panX, panY }` (zoom 1 and pans 0 when reset).
- **R2.20 Deck Builder makeover:** a dark stone war table lit by glowing crystals, cards with depth and soft shadows tilting slightly toward the pointer, an added card flies into the deck pile, the Oreheart logo at the top (`<img alt="Oreheart">`), ornate, no boxes.
