## Out of scope / limitations
- (Round 22) No new or regenerated 3D models; no changes to the Crystal Node tint, unit stat badge, ability icons or status icons (owner is editing them). Gnome and Dwarf models stay as they are.
- (Round 14) No new 3D models or Meshy calls; only the HUD layout, hand fans, flow shards and the two owner card changes. The AI's use of Brewlord is not checked automatically.
- (Round 12d) No new abilities, rule or layout changes, 3D models or Meshy calls. The owner's card format and renderer are used as delivered; changes to format.json itself are the owner's (Art Studio). Pixel-exact comparison with the reference JPGs is a manual check in the report, not an automated test.
- (Round 12b-1) Gnome and Dwarf models, Sproingle's and Borgo's thrones (Round 12b-2), other Goblin creatures' models and any rule or layout change are not in this round. Models not finished in time are reported and their checks skip.
- (Round 12a) New 3D models for Grubgrabber Miner, Boneknocker, Shiv Rat, Scrapsmith Miner and Grubnak, his junk throne and any Meshy call are Round 12b, not this build.

- (Round 11) Not in this round: the queued Menu Builder, deck builder, halls and card template work (Round 9b and queued 9c parts). Highlight/ring settings are per browser, not per account (no accounts exist).

- (Round 10) Only the 13 Round 10 items are built; Round 9b (in-game editor) and the queued Menu Builder / deck builder / halls work stay queued. The Crystal card rule (R10.12) is marked "for now" by the owner and may change later.

- (Round 9c) Round 9b (the in-game owner editor) is still not built. The Menu Builder page itself is not shipped; only its output file is used. "Quit" cannot close a browser tab, so it returns to the Intro screen.

- (Round 9a) Round 9b, the Owner Game Editor inside the game (`?editor=1`, handles, import/export, frames), is queued and deliberately not built. Gnome and Dwarf themes do not exist yet (placeholders).

- **Round 8b items are not in this build** (Commander facing, look-backs and impatience emotes; flipped card glow; tight hand hover box and no rise while dragging; 70 % pop-out; painted stat icons; Deck Builder drag-into-list and no-shift fixes; Choose Your Commander hover/backdrop/Change Commander and deck entries; Fight! bottom-right and removal of "View Grubnak in 3D"; brown-gold body text; close-up 3D viewer). Round 7 behavior stays for all of them.
- The arena GLB plan is dropped: the board is the owner's 2D painting with 3D pieces on top.

- (Round 6a) Round 6b / Build B (Meshy 3D models, starting with Grubnak) is not part of this round; the crystal mine uses the owner's placeholder glTF until a Meshy model replaces it. No rule changes in Round 6a.

- (Round 3b) Build B (Meshy 3D models, deck-builder 3D viewer) is not part of this round. No rule changes in Round 3b.

- (Round 2) New 3D creature models (Meshy, with Run animations) and the deck-builder 3D viewer are Build B, not this round; current figures are kept as they are.
- (Round 2) Voice search only works in browsers with SpeechRecognition (mainly Chrome); elsewhere the microphone button is hidden.
- (Round 2) Commander portraits and the title cavern background are generated offline with the image API (key never in the app); if generation fails, a painted fallback built from existing art is committed and the report says so.

- (Rev 9) The deck builder is still the testing version (no collection, packs or card ownership). Drag-to-pile is supported but only double-click and + are checked automatically.

- (Rev 7) Spell visual effects (R7.6) and card-reveal animations are checked by eye only; automated tests skip reveals in test mode.

- (Rev 8) The deck builder is a testing version: every card unlocked, no collection, packs or gold. Card reveal flips, spell effects, weapons on figures and the hand slide are checked by eye beyond the listed tests.
- (Phase 1B Round 1) Not yet: finished Gnome and Dwarf figures, finished Sproingle and Borgo, Gnome/Dwarf card pictures (Round 2); unique spell visual effects, WHAM!/KRAK!/ZZAP! hit starbursts, living menu scene, 3D Flow/Bank crystals, iron Done button (Round 3); music (later phase). The look (style match, animation quality, frame rate on real phones) is judged by the owner from screenshots and the preview; tests only check that the parts exist, sound is gated, Low reduces draw calls and size limits hold.
- (Phase 1B) The card-picture script needs the `XAI_API_KEY` secret at build time only; the app itself never calls an API or holds a key. Pictures are committed files.
- Not included: booster packs, collection, the collection-based deck builder, gold, shards, shop, the full 120-card Core Set, music, online play, accounts, Owner Console, cosmetics, daily quests, taunts, rankings, notifications, Hard AI, hint/undo buttons, time bank, sudden death (unresolved in the GDD), colorblind color picker, haptics settings, and PWA install/offline. The brief excludes them, or they need a backend.
- (Phase 1B) Settings has the graphics, moves-reminder and card-reveal toggles plus the two volume sliders; there is no music volume because there is no music yet.
- Unlockable Commanders (Queen Skreeka, Madame Sprocket, Old Grumblebeard) are not playable. Only the 3 starter Commanders are.
- There are no response chains (only one Respond level).
- (Rev 6) The crystal-bought upgrades (Sharpened Weapons, Heavy Armor, Veteran, Champion's Crown) are removed from the game, not just hidden.
- (Rev 8) Mixed-faction decks are allowed in the Deck Builder; a mine still glows in its controller's Commander faction color.
- The page can't force the phone to rotate. It only shows the rotate message.

---

- (Round 5a) The match screen layout, camera, hearts, crystal display, hand size and in-match controls are not changed this round (they belong to round 5b), apart from the new font and the card proportions.
- (Round 7) Only Grubnak gets a Meshy model this round; Sproingle, Borgo, all creatures and the mine keep their current models. The Meshy key is used only by the builder's one-off script at build time (the factory's "no secret keys" limit applies to the shipped app, which contains no key and calls no Meshy endpoint). If Meshy fails, Part B is reported as not done and its checks skip.

- (Round 8b) No new 3D models: only Grubnak's Commander card has a viewer; creatures, Sproingle and Borgo get models in later rounds. Meshy may be used only to add one or two clips to the existing rigged Grubnak.

## Assumptions
- (Round 26) The prompt's newest owner message is Round 15, but the branch's newest owner brief is **Round 26** (Oct 28, `oreheart-round-26-combined-brief.md`), which replaces the discontinued Rounds 24 and 25; this spec builds Round 26 and uses the brief's AC numbers (AC370–AC396). AC370 and AC371 were Round 24 skull-throw numbers; those checks are retired, so the numbers are reused. Checks cleanup: **3 removed** (the Round 24 skull-throw checks AC369/AC370 and AC371: the owner rejected the flying skull), **4 rewritten** (AC363: overlap 0.2 → 0.26; AC41: after a mine move Done no longer asks, and the drop-up text has no miner count; AC31 and AC37 now find the new drop-up by the pattern "Moves still available"), **27 test cases added** for the 26 new criteria. The brief's "previous build" size comparisons (bubble, Respond box) are checked through `getUiScales()`, which reports the old size, because a test can't see the old build. Bank-payment examples use Borgo and Shiv Rat (cost 4, no discount) so the brief's exact numbers hold. The brief says builders must not touch the acceptance folder; that rule is for builders, not for this plan.
- (Round 24) The prompt's newest owner message is Round 15, but the branch's newest owner brief is **Round 24** (Oct 26, "Skullflinger throws skulls that bonk off the target"); this spec builds Round 24. The brief numbers its checks AC358–AC360, but those numbers already belong to Round 22 checks, so the new checks are **AC369–AC371**. Checks cleanup: **1 removed** (AC356, a Round 21 screenshot-only test that timed out), **1 rewritten** (AC109: the enemy now gets crystals to pay for its summon), **2 added** (AC369/AC370 share one test because each throw needs a slow model load; AC371). The available results file (403/426) is from Round 22; Round 23's results were not available.
- (Round 24) The brief's "size unchanged" check can't compare against the original file from inside the browser, so AC371 checks the clips and `events.json` instead. The no-re-processing rule is stated in R24.
- (Round 24) The timing and bounce checks read the game's own `projectileLog` numbers (game clock), not screen frames, so they stay deterministic on a busy test machine.
- (Round 23) The prompt's newest owner message is Round 15, but the branch's newest owner brief is **Round 23** (Oct 25, commit "Owner brief: Round 23"); this spec builds Round 23. Checks cleanup: **0 removed, 3 rewritten, 6 added (AC363–AC368)**. Rewritten: **AC272** (dropped "other cards stay below the home row" and "every card ≥ 20 % visible beside the raised card" — the owner's layout editor now decides the fan: overlap 0.2 and "pop out fully" make both impossible); **AC355** (also allows Grubnak's own model download — Grubnak has a 3D model since round 12b-1, which made the check fail); **AC360** (time limit 30 s → 60 s; model load plus two full throws exceeded 30 s on the test machine). The owner's own updates to AC24, AC24b, AC80, AC84 and the Gnome/Dwarf "AC357" are kept as written (that owner check shares the number AC357 with the Round 22 controls check; both stay).
- (Round 23) Mine Control (AC341): the brief's Round 17 stance rule ("a Miner attacks an enemy Miner next to it only if it will survive the strike back; otherwise it mines; if low on crystals, it mines") makes **attack** correct for a Shield-1 Miner with 16 crystals, so the game is fixed and the check stays.
- (Round 23) The fan's numbers are checked through the new `getHandHover().fan` hook (rotation and unrotated size cannot be measured from bounding boxes), cross-checked against each card button's real on-screen box.

- (Round 22) The prompt's newest owner message is Round 15, but the branch's newest owner brief is **Round 22** (Oct 25, commit "Owner brief: Round 22"); this spec builds Round 22. Checks cleanup: **0 removed, 8 rewritten, 6 added (AC357–AC362)**. Rewritten because a newer owner decision or the owner's `cards.json` contradicts them: **AC98, AC116, AC250** (left-click now selects; right-click opens the card), **AC218** (it required the previous card to drop *below* the screen edge, but the resting hand is fully on screen since Round 17; now it must return to its resting place, plus the leave-the-fan rule), **AC272** (only the enlarged card may reach above the home row; the fan is closer), **AC13** (Grudge Hurler range 5, Skullflinger 6 from cards.json), **AC155** (Grubnak's −1 discount made the "too expensive" troll affordable; now a 1-cost Miner vs the troll), **AC89** (it placed a unit on row 0, which is not a board row; now row 2). The other failing checks are real game bugs (R22.4) and stay as they are.
- (Round 22) "Use the basketball shot for his special ability": his only activated ability, Strike, is described by the owner as the bowling roll, so `bowl` is used for both his attack and Strike, and `basketShot` is his entrance move when summoned (and may also play as a rarer idle taunt). The owner can redirect this later.
- (Round 22) Effect colors per faction (green/purple/blue/white) are checked by hue only, so the owner's own tint edits on the Crystal Node don't break the check.

- (Round 21) The newest owner request in the prompt is Round 15, but the branch's newest owner brief is Round 21 ("goblin 3D models in matches", Oct 25), which explicitly says to change nothing else and not rework old checks; this spec builds Round 21. Checks cleanup: **0 removed, 0 rewritten, 8 added (AC350–AC356; AC351 runs in two halves)**, per the owner's "do not rework old checks". `factory-design/models/` also holds `shiv-rat` and `goblin-smith-miner`; the brief counts them among the 4 already in the game, so the in-game versions are kept. Size limits (≤ 4 MB per file, ≤ 6 MB per folder) are my reading of "small enough to load quickly". Goblin units without an owner look get items placed by the builder from the model sheets; only bone names are checked for looked goblins.

- (Round 17 finish) The newest owner message in the prompt is Round 15, but Rounds 15–17 are already built and the owner has since changed the game directly (Oct 24 commits); those direct changes and their owner tests (`tests/round20.test.ts`) are treated as binding requests. Checks cleanup: **0 removed, 7 rewritten, 1 added (AC349)**. Rewritten because they contradicted the owner's current card data or newer rules, not the game: AC15 (the attacker `dwarf-miner` is now the unkillable Riot Shield Bearer → uses Ironjaw Big Back, drops the "attacker dies" line), AC143/AC204 (Gnome Plow, Grimhold Ravager and Ironjaw Big Back are hidden in a Goblin deck since Round 13 → Goblin cards), AC332 and AC335 (forgot Borgo's +1 Health → damage 6 / live numbers from `getState`), AC266 (Skullflinger is range 6 → uses Marksmen Gunner, range 7), AC109 (enemy Goblin card with faction lock off), AC260/AC261 (read settings hooks on a `?test=1` page, since R17.3 hides the hooks elsewhere).

- (Round 17) The prompt's feedback list ends at Round 15, but Rounds 15 and 16 are already built and the brief's newest section, "Round 17 … read this first", is the owner's current request; this spec plans Round 17.
- (Round 17) Checks cleanup: 0 tests deleted; about 30 tests rewritten — every `Start` button click/lookup now uses "Fight!" (R17.1 says the visible button is Fight! and the hidden Start must go), and 9 hand checks (AC43, AC163, AC174, AC175, AC251 and 4 others) changed from "tucked to a quarter at rest" to "fully on screen at rest" because the owner now says the resting hand must not sit mostly below the bottom edge. 11 new tests added (AC338–AC348).
- (Round 17) Stance details the owner left open: units act in reading order; "low on crystals" = Flow + Bank pays for fewer than half of a non-empty hand; "near" for Guard = Chebyshev distance ≤ 2; Aggressive moves reduce Chebyshev distance to the enemy stronghold; Mine Control survival uses the same math as `previewAttack`. In test mode a `newMatch()` call always starts in Standard unless its `mode` option says otherwise, so older checks are unaffected by a remembered mode; the Fight! button uses the remembered mode.
- (Round 17) "Exit to menu" in Card Comp vs Card Comp leaves without a confirmation (it is a watch-only match). The Card Play speed toggle offers 1× and 2×, the watch bar 1×, 2× and 4×.

- (Round 15) Checks cleanup: **1 test removed in effect and 7 rewritten**, no new duplicates. AC167's palette check became the Round 15 "all text outside cards is white" scan (gold/brown text is now forbidden); AC135 and AC137 now expect white menu text instead of #FFD700; AC326 (both tests) expects white HUD text instead of the layout's colors; AC293 and AC320 now expect the sideways bowling-pin knock instead of knock-back (AC293 gained a second test for the new card text). Old round screenshot-only tests were already removed in Round 14. Facing is a compass string with N = toward row 1; the knock distance and side come from the seeded RNG; Commanders are never knocked; badges are DOM overlays inside the creature label so they are testable without pixels.
- (Round 14) Checks cleanup: **24 tests removed** and **14 rewritten**. Removed because later owner rounds replaced what they checked: AC57 (short text = full text; owner text is now long), AC65 (old weapon table), AC66/AC202 (duplicates of AC30), AC81/AC83 (old `/art/cards/*.webp` pictures; owner paintings now), AC91/AC118/AC119/AC120/AC132/AC147 (old faction frames, replaced by the owner card format in 12d), AC92 (80-character short text), AC94 (old Commander art path), AC96 (ornate buttons; buttons are plain text since 5a), AC107/AC139 (Deck Builder logo and panel side; logo removed in 13), AC210 (brown-gold body text; the Deck Builder is white since 9c/11), AC214 (exact byte size of the bank picture), AC220 (standing Grubnak's scepter; he sits on his throne since 12b-1), AC255 (old short-text wording), and the old round 3b / 8b / 12b-1 screenshot-only tests. Rewritten to the owner's current card data: AC10 (Mangewolf Rider costs 8), AC16 (Dwarf Smith Miners mine; Thunderhorn Blast costs 3), AC18 (Snuff Out vs the 5-cost Emergency Rations; Pickpocket! refund math), AC30 (Mangewolf Movement 3), AC131 (Venom Shiv 7 turns, no healing), AC175/AC218 (hand ≈ 34 % of the frame, fanned), AC212 (Oct 24 layout bytes), AC248 (Skullflinger range 6), AC296 (Rocket Rider is a Fighter), AC307 (Flow is spent before the Bank), AC60/AC209/AC239 (the Deck Builder lists only the chosen Commander's faction plus neutral cards, so they use Venom Shiv and switch to Knobbin for Gnome cards).
- (Round 14) Fan angles, the Drunk state and shard counts are exposed as `data-fan-angle`, `data-drunk`, `drunk`, `flowShardCount()` and `getFlowStreams().pulses` so the checks can read them without pixels. "Rounds" for Drunk = the owner's turns (counts down at the start of the owner's turn). "Adjacent" = the 8 surrounding squares. "Dwarf" = a Dwarf-faction creature. Brewlord can't target itself.

- (Round 13) Ability buttons, the Deck Builder Commander portraits and the title card stack use the exact accessible names and `data-` attributes written in R13.2–R13.4 so the checks can find them. "Sproingle" in the brief is the Commander id `sproingle`, displayed with the owner's current name "Knobbin the Gnomeificent". When the Commander is switched in the Deck Builder, already-added cards the new Commander can't use stay in the deck and show as problems instead of being removed silently.
- (Round 12d) Faces keep their old accessible labels (Cost, Attack, Health, Movement, Type, `data-frame`) so existing checks keep working; where the format has no layer for one (e.g. no type line on spells) the label sits on a visually hidden element. The card back everywhere is the format's `back` layout. Text inside card faces is exempt from the one-font rule because the owner's format sets its own fonts.

- (Round 12c, owner questions — simplest reading chosen) Ranged cards whose text says "any direction" still use the straight-line rule; Rocket Rider's explosion hits friend and foe but not Commanders and costs nothing; Thief steals from Flow, then Bank; Gemdrunk's bonus follows the live Bank; Riot Shield lasts its owner's first 3 turns; Brawler Adept's "can't cast spells" has no game effect; Overclocked Aethervein's "extra Crystal cards" means the second and later Crystal card played in one turn; Dwarves have no basic Miner, so Dwarf starter decks use 50 Anvilsworn Miners; Gearward's assist deals its Attack to the attacker.
- (Round 12b-1) The owner sheets show several views in one picture, so the builder crops them into separate images for Meshy's multi-image job. Props may be baked into the mesh or built as separate small props, as long as they are visible and match the sheet. The new seated Grubnak keeps the file name `grubnak.glb` so the existing viewer and select-screen checks still apply. The throne faces the same way as Grubnak (146°) because he sits in it. Any canonical animation Meshy can't supply for a seated figure is built in code (marked `procedural`).

- (Round 10) "Crystal cards any time" means any moment the player can act (own Deploy and Action phases, and as a responder in the Respond window); the game gives the human no other input during the enemy's turn.
- (Round 10) For Ranged lines, Strongholds count as units (they block and can be the target, meaning the Commander); mines and empty squares never block.
- (Round 10) "Basic Miner" includes the free Miner; death from any cause draws, and an empty deck simply draws nothing.
- (Round 10) "Twice the size" of the card close-up is capped by the screen height, so the face fills 80–100 % of the window height.

- (Round 9c) The owner menus file wins over older menu wording: the main menu is Play / Deck Builder / How to Play / Settings / Quit, the select screen's start button was "Start" (Round 17: it is "Fight!" again, the only start button), and the Tutorial is reached from How to Play (Rules page) or the Welcome offer. "How to Play" has no target in the file; it opens the Rules page. The difficulty buttons live inside the `cDecks` zone.
- (Round 9c) The Intro plays once per browser tab (a reload goes straight to the main menu; `?intro=1` forces it) so repeated reloads are not slowed by a 7-second intro; in test mode it is skipped unless `?intro=1`.
- (Round 9c) Because browsers block sound before the first click, intro sounds can only be heard after a user gesture; the cues are still logged in `sfxLog` and play when audio is allowed.

- (Round 9a) The owner's six Menu items replace the old pop-out items; Last turn, Mute, Card pop-ups and Recenter moved into "Settings" → "Match settings" so nothing is lost. "Give up match!" replaces "Surrender".
- (Round 9a) Windows that are not 16:9 use a height-fitted (or width-fitted) 16:9 HUD frame with each piece anchored to its nearest edge by its layout center (left/center/right thirds), as the brief's "anchored to their nearest screen edge".
- (Round 9a) The hand card size is the layout hand piece height (33.81 % of the frame) and the raised card is 1.6 × that, so the R8b "~70 % pop-out" no longer applies.
- (Round 9a) Mine height follows the owner layout (1.7 squares × scale 1.46 ≈ 2.48), replacing the old 1.5.
- (Round 9a) Corner frames are drawn from the player's theme only; the opponent's theme affects its crystal, bank and colors.

- (Round 8a) The default/Recenter view anchors the painting's top edge when the window is wider than 16:9 (the extra strip is cut from the bottom), so the far Commander stays whole; horizontally it is centered.
- (Round 8a) `zoom` in `getCamera()` is relative to the cover-fit scale, so zoom 1 still means "fully zoomed out" for the older checks.
- (Round 8a) The Meshy mine lives in its own `public/models/mines/models.json` rather than the Grubnak file.
- (Round 8a) Owner files are copied into `public/art/arena/` with unchanged names so the checks can compare bytes.
- (Round 7) "Popped" = at least 80 % of the window height and "rest size" = at most 47 %, so the stuck-card bug is testable. The window-leave case is specified but not automatically tested (Playwright cannot move the pointer outside the page).
- (Round 7) The owner's card back is stored as `public/art/ui/oreheart-card-back.jpg` (keeping the file name the existing checks look for) and the deck pile as `public/art/ui/deck-pile.png`, both byte-identical copies of the owner files.
- (Round 7) "Same thickness" is checked through the `[data-card-glow]` element and `getHandGlow()` hooks, with 2 px tolerance; color families are checked by channel order, not exact hex values.
- (Round 7) The 8 canonical animation names (`idle`, `laugh`, `point`, `celebrate`, `surprised`, `cast`, `hit`, `defeat`) are the game's own names; `models.json` maps each to the Meshy library clip actually used.

- (Round 6a) The hovered hand card itself is the 2× pop-out (as in AC59), so "about 85–90 % of the screen height" is checked on that card (80–92 % accepted). The hand is centered on the screen so the enemy hand can mirror it; the R5b "first card at 14 %" rule is dropped.
- (Round 6a) All text buttons stay bright gold (`--text-gold`) so the plain-text button checks keep working; "Medium 500" applies to secondary buttons and labels.
- (Round 6a) Palette variable names (`--text-gold`, `--text-brown`, `--text-brown-light`, `--text-white`, `--text-soft`, `--text-black`, `--text-ink`, `--color-player`, `--color-enemy`, `--color-warning`, `--color-success`) were chosen so tests can check them; text inside card faces keeps the card designs' faction colors.
- (Round 6a) "Nudge the deck up slightly" is read as the deck's top between 62 % and 84 % of the height.

- (Round 5a) Menu buttons keep their exact names so older checks still find them; only their look changes. A chosen difficulty or other selected choice is shown by a stronger text glow, not a box.
- (Round 5a) The Deck Builder action stays labelled "New Deck" (as in the older checks); the carousel slot reads "+ New Deck" and does the same thing.
- (Round 5a) "Deck pile" now means the block holding the deck carousel plus the big name and count; dropping a card anywhere on it (or on the deck list) adds one copy.
- (Round 5a) "About 8 rows" in the deck list is checked as at least 8 rows at 1280×720 and at least 7 on an 844×390 phone, where the screen is very short.
- (Round 5a) A copied starter deck is saved immediately and counts toward the 5-deck limit. Removing a starter deck only hides it on this device.
- (Round 5a) Red deck problems, white Health numbers on hearts and red damaged Health keep their colors. They are deliberate signals, not "secondary text".

- (Round 3b) "Precisely centered", "smaller" and "uniform" are tested with tolerances (centered within 20 % of the slot, numbers ≤ 10 % of card height, crystal 3–12 %, names up to 16 characters identical). The look itself (bevel, facets, filigree) is checked in the report screenshots.
- (Round 3b) Custom dropdowns use the ARIA combobox/listbox pattern so they stay accessible and testable; native inputs may remain underneath custom visuals if visually hidden.
- (Round 3b) The painted Deck Builder background is generated once at build time with the image API (as for earlier art) and shipped as a static file; no key is used at runtime.
- (Round 3a) Starter deck names drop "The" ("Goblin Horde", not "Goblin The Horde") for readable name plates. All nine starter decks are offered to every Commander (decks may mix factions); the Commander's own faction's three come first.
- (Round 3a) "About N Miners" is tested as a range (Warband 7–9, Arcane Hoard 10–14, Horde 12–16). The Warband keeps the exact 5 basic + 3 Smith Miners already tested by AC6.
- (Round 3a) In test mode the opponent defaults to its Warband so older tests stay deterministic; only normal play picks the AI deck at random (seeded).
- (Round 3a) The test-only `mines` option no longer has to be mirrored, so older tests can keep their fixed layout (4,2), (4,4), (4,6).

- (Round 2) Neutral cards have no owner frame; they use a grey-silver recolor of an owner frame (`card-frame-neutral`).
- (Round 2) `text` is kept as an alias of `shortText` so older hooks and checks still work; the full wording lives in `longText`.
- (Round 2) Mulligan math: the 2nd and 3rd mulligans redraw one card fewer than were swapped, which gives the GDD's 5 → 5 → 4 → 3 when cards are swapped each time.
- (Round 2) The Display mode control is a radio group, not buttons, so the title screen keeps exactly the five owner-approved buttons.
- (Round 2, superseded in Round 3b) Deck Builder filters were native selects; Round 3b replaces them with accessible custom comboboxes (R3b.6) because a native dropdown list cannot be styled.
- (Round 2) `&fx=1` exists only so automated checks can see real reveal timing in test mode; it has no effect outside test mode.
- (Round 2) "Short" card text is capped at 80 characters.

- (Phase 1B) "Goblin cards" for figures and pictures means every creature card with faction `goblins`; Goblin-faction spells/Crystal cards (if any) and neutral cards keep their old pictures this round.
- (Phase 1B) The arena reference shows stat badges above creatures; the approved R8.9 compact stat pill under each creature stays (layout and tests are frozen), restyled with the dark badge look and sword/heart/boot icons.
- (Phase 1B) Outside assets live under `public/assets/external/` so the credits check can find them; assets made in code need no credit.
- (Phase 1B) Volume sliders default to 80; mute is a match-screen button only (the main menu keeps exactly five buttons).
- (Phase 1B) Stronghold heart cracks appear at Health ≤ 14 and grow at ≤ 7.

- (Rev 9) Savings moves floor(Flow/10) crystals (1 for 10–19), so Flow drops by the amount saved, not by 10 per crystal: "1 crystal for every 10 moves from your Flow into your Bank".
- (Rev 9) The deck builder now blocks adding beyond a card's copy limit (the brief says + "respects limits"); the Crystal-card total is reported as a problem rather than blocked, so the R8 message still appears.
- (Rev 9) `tutorialAction()` is a test-only hook so the automated checks can play the whole tutorial without dragging in 3D; it mirrors the tutorial's own tips.
- (Rev 9) The last-used picks are saved when "Fight!" is pressed (not when merely tapped).

- (Rev 8) Collector cards are limited to 3 copies, following the design doc (the brief said 1; the design doc wins). The limits live in config (`copyLimits`, `maxCrystalCards`), so the owner can change them.
- (Rev 8) Unfinished or illegal decks can be saved as drafts so testers don't lose work, but only legal decks can be picked for a match.
- (Rev 8) Tapping a mine uses all ready Miners next to *that* mine, even if it is enemy-owned (half pay): the player chose the mine. Tapping a mine with no ready Miners shows its info instead.
- (Rev 8) Auto-skip only acts on the human player's phases; the AI ends its own phases, and in tests an opponent driven by `act()` is never skipped, so tests stay deterministic.
- (Rev 8) A Crystal card that would make a card affordable does not stop the Deploy auto-skip (the brief says Crystal cards never hold it up).
- (Rev 8) "Below half Health" (Rage) means current Health × 2 < max Health. Runic Siphoner's Bank crystal triggers even if it is defeated in the same exchange.

- (Rev 7) Mine ownership for the half-pay rule is computed live from creatures with `minedMine` set (both sides), so the preview and Mine All see it; the payout uses ownership recomputed at the receiving player's Draw phase (as in Revision 5).
- (Rev 7) The player's mixed-deck/Commander color only affects how crystals look; it has no rules effect.
- (Rev 7) A basic Miner played as an upgrade is never free, even when the free-Miner condition holds; the free Miner only applies to summons.
- (Rev 7) Older lines in this spec that mention colors (green/blue/purple/white crystals, half value off-color, `colors` for Heart of the Fallen) or old card names are superseded by R7.1 and R7.9; `sites[].color` remains only for the mine glow.

- (Rev 6) Basic Miner card ids stay `goblin-scrounger`, `gnome-prospector`, `dwarf-miner` (only display names change), so saved data and tests keep working.
- (Rev 6) "Flow first, then Bank" is applied per payment: Flow is drained as far as it usefully goes, and Bank covers the rest. Spell gains (Prospector's Prayer) count as Flow; only Crystal cards feed the Bank.
- (Rev 6) A Smith upgrade is paid exactly like summoning that Smith card (Commander creature discounts apply) and is allowed in the Deploy phase only, matching the GDD's "Deploy: play Crystal cards and Smith upgrades".
- (Rev 6) "Matching" Instant = one that could legally take effect on the triggering action if it were affordable. A Respond window with nothing affordable is still shown so the player sees what they're missing.
- (Rev 6) The center-screen phase banners stay; the new top-right turn display is the always-visible "Current phase" element.
- (Rev 6) The old line "8 basic Gatherers per deck" below is replaced by 5 basic Miners + 3 Smith Miners.
- (Rev 6) Crystal cards in the Respond window are limited by the same one-per-round counter, so a player who played one on their own turn can't play another on the enemy's turn.

- (Rev 5) Mined crystals take the mine's color as recomputed at the receiving player's Draw phase, and only miners still on the battlefield and adjacent pay out; this makes the GDD example (1 Gnome vs 2 Goblin miners → green) exact for both sides.
- (Rev 5) "At least 2 spaces apart" means Chebyshev distance ≥ 2. Mirroring is by row (3 ↔ 5, same column), as the brief says.
- (Rev 5) Mining needs an unused action: Charge creatures can't combine mining with moving or attacking.
- (Rev 5) The hover pop-out is checked as "moves up ≥ 30 px"; the exact 1-inch size is a visual target.

- The player is at the bottom (rows 6–7) and the computer at the top (rows 1–2), with coordinates as in 3.1.
- "The second player draws 1 extra card" means one extra card right after the mulligan (opening hand of 6), plus their normal turn draw.
- Healing, losing old Shield and start-of-turn abilities happen in the Draw phase, before income. Setup matches skip the turn-1 Draw phase entirely.
- Mine colors are recomputed for all mines at the start of every turn; only the active player's pool is reset there.
- For a creature touching two mines, the brief and GDD give slightly different tie-breaks; the spec uses one fixed rule (3.2: more own creatures, then more enemy creatures, then the left mine) so tests are exact.
- Crystal Eaters count as Gatherers for flow (2), but not as basic Gatherers for the swap or free Gatherer.
- When the pool is over the cap, white and off-color crystals are dropped first.
- `setup` crystals are not capped, so tests can give large amounts.
- The starter deck mix stays 40/6/4 with 8 basic Gatherers: with the free Gatherer on turn 1 and the Gatherer swap, a player usually has flow and can afford something by their third turn.
- Deck-out damage is a flat 1 per failed draw (not escalating), and it is configurable.
- Spells are paid with white and non-faction colors first. Fizzle checks the printed cost (≤3).
- Guard forces melee attackers adjacent to a Guard creature to target a Guard creature. It does not block Ranged attacks.
- Ranged creatures may attack the enemy Commander when within Chebyshev distance 2 of the enemy Stronghold space. All Ranged attacks (even adjacent ones) get no strike back.
- "Moves left" (for auto-skip and the pop-up) also ignores surrender. Casting a Regular spell counts as a move; casting an Instant doesn't.
- The free Gatherer applies only to the three basic Gatherers (not Crystal Eaters), so it can't be used to get an expensive Gatherer for free. "0 crystals" means 0 of every color.
- The Gatherer swap takes the first basic Gatherer found from the top of the deck. All three factions have only one basic Gatherer, so "cheapest" is always that card.
- Phase banners are shown only for the human's own phases plus "ENEMY TURN"; the enemy's individual phases get no banner.
- The income preview uses the current board positions, because creatures may still move before the next turn.
- Auto-skip is off by default in test mode so that tests can set up a position and inspect it. It is on in normal play and in the tutorial.
- "Back row full" counts all 6 non-Stronghold spaces of the row, occupied by either side.
- Charge creatures may move and then attack. A creature that attacked can't move afterwards.
- Shield points stack and leftover Shield ends at the start of the owner's next turn. Shield Bubble remains its own effect (it stops the next hit this turn).
- Starter decks may include Rares/Ultra Rares/Collectors within the copy limits, so all 41 cards are playable in 1a.
- The first-time tutorial offer, the graphics preference and the "seen tutorial" flag are stored in localStorage under keys the builder chooses.

**Round 4 assumptions:**
- The owner's spell frames (`spell-frame-*`) aren't in the repository yet, so the build uses close temporary copies that can be swapped by replacing the files.
- (Round 4, superseded) Chosen fonts were Cinzel and Tinos; Round 5a replaces them with Alegreya SC Italic only.
- Decks had no Commander before, so the Deck Builder gets a "Deck Commander" dropdown to decide which faction spells are locked. Locked spells are shown dimmed (not hidden) so the owner can still see them.
- "Can't heal while poisoned" also blocks Field Bandage and the start-of-turn heal. Poison ticks before that heal would happen.
- The casting lock can be switched off in test mode only (`factionLock: false`), so the older automated checks that hand-pick spells keep working. Real matches always use the lock.
- Starter decks drop the old "at most 4 copies of any other card" rule, because 46 Fighters from one faction need more copies. The rarity copy limits still apply.
- (Round 8b) "Only his decks" means his faction's starter decks plus saved decks whose Deck Commander is him; the "last deck can't be removed" rule now applies per Commander. The select screen opens in the row state with the remembered/default Commander selected, so existing flows that click one Commander and press Fight! keep working. The Deck Builder gets a small "Commander card" tile so Grubnak's card (not a deck card) can be opened in the close-up with the viewer. Impatience and look-back timing get a test-mode `advanceIdle` hook and random look-backs are off in test mode to keep checks deterministic.
- (Round 11) The "Click to begin" gate is shown outside test mode whenever the Intro plays; in test mode it needs `&begin=1` so older Intro checks (`intro=1`) keep working unchanged.
- (Round 11) Highlight and ring settings are saved per browser (there are no accounts); "per player" means this browser's profile. Color settings left at `null` follow whichever faction the player is using in that match.
- (Round 11) The 3 px tolerance on the mine button lets the button be a rectangle around the square's slightly trapezoid quad.
- (Round 11) "Shot stops at the first unit" counts creatures and Strongholds of both sides; mines are not units and don't block shots.
- (Round 16) Checks cleanup: **0 tests removed, 3 rewritten**, no new tests (the brief asks for no new features). AC238 now expects a white deck name with a Goblin-green glow/tint (Round 15 made all non-card text white, so the old "green name" contradicted it); AC310 accepted only a list of layout names but the spec allows an object keyed by name (the check called `toContain` on an object, which can never pass); AC326 at 844×390 now uses max(layout size, 14 px), because the older passing AC158 (no text under 14 px on phones) and the layout size conflicted. All other 67 failures are treated as game bugs.
